Intelligent services in this context refer to privacy-sensitive distributed decision systems, such as fraud detection and genomic classification, where reliable and fair client-level learning is as important as the accuracy of the aggregate model. However, heterogeneous client data and noisy quantum optimization often cause unstable local updates, client drift, and unfair performance between clients. This paper proposes DUQFL-Prox, a drift-stable quantum federated learning framework based on deep-unfolded local optimization. Instead of using a fixed local optimizer, each client performs adaptive unfolded SPSA updates, while a proximal term keeps the local model close to the global model. A lightweight controller learns step-specific optimization parameters to improve post-aggregation performance. Experiments on financial fraud and genomic classification tasks show that DUQFL-Prox improves stability, generalization, and client fairness compared with standard QFL baselines. The results suggest that deep-unfolded quantum federated learning can support more reliable and fair intelligent services in heterogeneous distributed environments.

中文摘要翻译: 量子联邦学习使得分布式客户端能够在无需共享本地数据的情况下训练量子神经网络，因此有望用于隐私感知的智能服务。此处的智能服务指隐私敏感的分布式决策系统，如欺诈检测和基因组分类，在这些系统中，可靠且公平的客户端级学习与聚合模型的准确性同等重要。然而，异构的客户端数据和带噪声的量子优化常导致局部更新不稳定、客户端漂移以及客户端间性能不公平。本文提出DUQFL-Prox——一种基于深度展开局部优化的漂移稳定量子联邦学习框架。该框架中，每个客户端不使用固定的局部优化器，而是执行自适应的展开SPSA更新，同时通过一个近端项使局部模型保持接近全局模型。一个轻量级控制器学习每步特定的优化参数以提升聚合后的性能。在金融欺诈和基因组分类任务上的实验表明，与标准QFL基线相比，DUQFL-Prox在稳定性、泛化性和客户端公平性方面均有提升。结果表明，深度展开的量子联邦学习能够在异构分布式环境中支持更可靠、更公平的智能服务。

</details>


### [85] [Shallower ReLU Network Representations via Exact Linear Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21651)
*Kilian Rueß,Gennadiy Averkov,Florestan Brunck,Moritz Grillo,Christoph Hertrich,Georg Loho,Jack Stade,Moritz Stargalla,Matthew Sun,Martin Winter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于n≤10，n个实数的最大值可通过两个隐层的ReLU网络精确表示；对于更大的n，给出了上界为⌈log₅(n/2)⌉+1个隐层的表示方法，并推广到所有连续分段线性函数。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 研究ReLU网络精确表示最大值函数所需的最小深度，这是一个关于神经网络表达能力的基础理论问题，旨在超越Bakaev等人（STOC'26）关于max₅的双隐层表示及maxₙ的⌈log₃(n-2)⌉+1层上界的结果。

Method: 将问题归约为有理数上的精确线性代数：通过对称性约化，将所需的消元过程编码为Q上的有限线性系统，并用计算方法求解验证。关键思路是构造结构化第一隐层（仅含成对最大值），使其可递归代入更大网络。

Result: (1) max₁₀存在双隐层ReLU表示；(2) 对任意n>10，maxₙ可用⌈log₅(n/2)⌉+1 < log₅(n)+1.5694个隐层表示；(3) 该深度界推广到R^d上的连续分段线性函数（以d+1替代n）；(4) d≤9时，R^d上所有连续分段线性函数均有双隐层ReLU表示。

Conclusion: 本文改进了Bakaev等人的结果，将双隐层可表示的最大规模从5提升到10，并将maxₙ的隐层上界从⌈log₃(n-2)⌉+1改进为⌈log₅(n/2)⌉+1。

Original Abstract: We prove that the maximum of $n$ real numbers is exactly representable by a ReLU network with two hidden layers for every $n\le 10$. The constructions are obtained by reducing the problem to exact rational linear algebra: after a symmetry reduction, the necessary cancellations are encoded in finite linear systems over $\mathbb{Q}$, which we solve and verify computationally. The representation of $\max_{10}$ has a structured first hidden layer consisting only of pairwise maxima, a feature that allows it to be recursively substituted into larger networks. We use this to show that for every $n>10$, the maximum $\max_{n}$ can be exactly represented with $\lceil{\log_5 (n / 2)\rceil}+1 < \log_5(n) +1.5694$ hidden layers. Via the generalized hinging-hyperplane representation [Wang, Sun, IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory 2005], the same depth bound holds for all continuous piecewise-linear functions on $\mathbb{R}^d$, with $d+1$ in place of $n$. In particular, every continuous piecewise-linear function on $\mathbb{R}^d$ for $d\le 9$ admits a two-hidden-layer ReLU representation. Our results improve on [Bakaev, Brunck, Hertrich, Stade, Yehudayoff, STOC'26]. In that work, the authors established a two-hidden-layer representation for $\max_{5}$ and an upper bound of $\lceil{\log_3 (n-2)\rceil}+1$ hidden layers for $\max_{n}$.

中文摘要翻译: 我们证明了对于每个n≤10，n个实数的最大值都可以通过一个两隐层ReLU网络精确表示。该构造通过将问题归约为精确有理线性代数得到：经过对称性约化后，必要的消元过程被编码为Q上的有限线性系统，我们通过计算求解并验证了这些系统。max₁₀的表示具有结构化的第一隐层，仅由成对最大值构成，这一特性使其可以递归地代入更大规模的网络。我们利用这一点表明，对于每个n>10，最大值maxₙ可以用⌈log₅(n/2)⌉+1 < log₅(n)+1.5694个隐层精确表示。通过广义铰链-超平面表示[Wang, Sun, IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory 2005]，相同的深度上界也适用于R^d上的所有连续分段线性函数，只需将n替换为d+1。特别地，对于d≤9，R^d上的每个连续分段线性函数都允许一个两隐层ReLU表示。我们的结果改进了Bakaev, Brunck, Hertrich, Stade, Yehudayoff在STOC'26上的工作。在该工作中，作者建立了max₅的两隐层表示以及maxₙ的⌈log₃(n-2)⌉+1个隐层的上界。

</details>


### [86] [Molt: A Scalable PyTorch-Native Training Framework for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21653)
*Jian Hu,Huiying Li,Hao Zhang,Binfeng Xu,Yifan Zhang,Shaokun Zhang,Hemil Desai,Michael Demoret,Pavlo Molchanov,Jan Kautz,Yi Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Molt是一个PyTorch原生的强化学习训练框架，以极简代码库实现端到端算法追踪与修改，支持异步多模态和混合专家策略训练，在性能上与基于Megatron的SOTA框架相当。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有强化学习框架中，每次算法修改（如新的估计器、流水线阶段、rollout方案）都需要穿透训练器、分布式后端和rollout胶水层进行修改，导致研究者的迭代成本高昂。Molt旨在降低这一成本。

Method: Molt采用PyTorch原生的极简代码库设计，使研究者能够完全掌握代码逻辑，AI编码助手也能完整阅读和理解。它将智能体视为普通程序，通过一个异步循环训练多模态和混合专家策略，确保不会在非智能体生成的token上进行训练，保持token、策略版本和模型语义的一致性。

Result: 在匹配的完全异步协议下，Molt与基于Megatron的SOTA框架在性能上具有统计可比性，证明了简洁性不会牺牲性能。

Conclusion: Molt是一个开源的高效强化学习训练框架，兼顾了代码的简洁性和高性能，并提供了完整的recipes和容器支持。

Original Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning research is constant algorithm modification, new estimators, new pipeline stages, new rollout schemes, and in mainstream frameworks each change threads through layers of trainer, distributed backend, and rollout glue: the cost lands on the researcher at every iteration. Molt is a PyTorch-native training framework built to keep that cost small: a codebase compact and clean enough for a researcher to hold in their head, and for an AI coding assistant to read and reason about in its entirety, so the algorithm flow can be traced and changed end to end. The agent is an ordinary program, and one asynchronous loop trains multimodal and mixture-of-experts policies while never training on a token it did not generate, consistent in tokens, policy versions, and model semantics. Leanness does not cost performance: under a matched, fully asynchronous protocol, Molt is statistically comparable to a state-of-the-art Megatron-based stack. Molt is open source and provides recipes and containers at https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/labs-molt.

中文摘要翻译: 智能体强化学习研究涉及持续的算法修改、新估计器、新流水线阶段和新rollout方案，而在主流框架中，每一次修改都需要穿透训练器、分布式后端和rollout胶水层：这一成本在每个迭代周期都落在研究者身上。Molt是一个PyTorch原生的训练框架，旨在保持这一成本低廉：其代码库紧凑而清晰，足以让研究者完全掌握，也足以让AI编码助手完整阅读和推理，从而使得算法流程可以被端到端地追踪和修改。智能体是一个普通程序，通过一个异步循环训练多模态和混合专家策略，同时确保不会在非智能体生成的token上进行训练，在token、策略版本和模型语义上保持一致。简洁性不会牺牲性能：在匹配的完全异步协议下，Molt与基于Megatron的先进框架在统计上具有可比性。Molt是开源的，并在https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/labs-molt提供recipes和容器。

</details>


### [87] [Physically Constrained Federated Additive Models for O-RAN SLA-Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21665)
*Aubida A. Al-Hameed,Mohammed M. H. Qazzaz,Maryam Hafeez,Syed A. Zaidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Monotone FedNAM，一种物理约束的联邦可加模型，用于O-RAN中可审计的跨基站切片SLA违规预测，在保持模型可解释性的同时消除违背无线物理规律的单调性违反，并将上行流量降低65%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: O-RAN中 proactive 服务保障需要预测切片SLA违规，但预测模型必须可审计（operator可审查），且训练数据不能跨基站池化（切片KPI具有商业敏感性）。已有的神经可加模型（NAM）虽可审计，但未受约束的NAM会学到违背无线物理规律的效果（如信道质量变好时预测风险反而升高），这一问题在本地训练、集中训练和非IID联邦平均下均存在。

Method: 提出Monotone FedNAM，一种联邦可加模型：将具有明确物理方向性的KPI表示为单调样条（monotone splines），其约束经过FedAvg聚合后仍能保持；而存在争议的KPI保持无约束。模型作为Non-RT RIC rApp训练，可紧凑部署为Near-RT RIC xApp。

Result: 在ColO-RAN测试台数据集上：消除所有单调性违反；受约束形状一致性从0.71提升至1.00；可泛化至未见过的调度策略；上行流量降低65%；AUC仅损失0.04-0.07。

Conclusion: 物理约束的联邦可加模型能够支持多租户O-RAN服务保障中可审计的SLA风险推断，在保持可解释性的同时确保物理合理性并显著降低通信开销。

Original Abstract: Proactive service assurance in O-RAN requires predicting per-slice SLA violations before they occur. The prediction model must be auditable by operators and must train across base stations without pooling per-slice KPIs, which are commercially sensitive because slices are leased to individual tenants. Neural additive models (NAMs) offer auditability because each KPI contributes through a visible shape function. However, visibility alone does not guarantee physical validity. On the ColO-RAN testbed dataset, unconstrained NAMs learn effects that contradict wireless physics, for example predicting higher risk when channel quality improves. This failure appears under both local and centralized training, and non-IID federated averaging worsens it. We present Monotone FedNAM, a federated additive model in which KPIs with unambiguous physical direction are represented as monotone splines whose constraints survive FedAvg aggregation by construction, while contestable KPIs remain unconstrained. The model trains and operates as a Non-RT RIC rApp and is compact enough for deployment as a Near-RT RIC xApp. Monotone FedNAM eliminates all monotonicity violations, raises constrained shape consistency from 0.71 to 1.00, generalizes to an unseen scheduling policy, and reduces uplink traffic by 65%, at a cost of 0.04 to 0.07 AUC. These results show that physically constrained federated additive models can support auditable SLA risk inference for multi-tenant O-RAN service assurance

中文摘要翻译: O-RAN中的主动服务保障要求在SLA违规发生之前预测每个切片的违规情况。预测模型必须可由运营商审计，并且必须跨基站训练，但不能池化每个切片的KPI，因为这些KPI具有商业敏感性——切片是租用给单个租户的。神经可加模型（NAM）提供可审计性，因为每个KPI通过可见的形状函数贡献。然而，仅可见性并不能保证物理有效性。在ColO-RAN测试台数据集上，未受约束的NAM学习到了违背无线物理规律的效果，例如在信道质量改善时预测更高的风险。这种失败在本地训练和集中训练下均会出现，而非独立同分布的联邦平均使其恶化。我们提出Monotone FedNAM，一种联邦可加模型，其中具有明确物理方向性的KPI被表示为单调样条，其约束通过FedAvg聚合后仍然保持，而存在争议的KPI则保持无约束。该模型作为Non-RT RIC rApp训练和运行，并且足够紧凑，可部署为Near-RT RIC xApp。Monotone FedNAM消除了所有单调性违反，将受约束形状一致性从0.71提升至1.00，可泛化至未见过的调度策略，并将上行流量降低65%，AUC仅损失0.04-0.07。这些结果表明，物理约束的联邦可加模型能够支持多租户O-RAN服务保障中可审计的SLA风险推断。

</details>


### [88] [Neural Feature Governance: Extending Atom Prevalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21671)
*Idris Karel Seunda Ekwe,Patrick Tenga Shako,Ernest Parfait Fokoué*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经原子盛行度（NAP）框架，通过贝叶斯结构化节点级模型选择，同时实现前馈神经网络的稀疏性、准确性、可解释性和不确定性量化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现代深度学习中，数十亿参数的架构虽然精度高，但缺乏透明性、计算效率和可靠的不确定性量化，神经网络的压缩与可解释性仍是开放挑战。

Method: NAP是一个四阶段混合框架：①通过迭代幅度剪枝（IMP）识别贝叶斯彩票（BLT）；②对Spike and Slab独立高斯（SS-IG）模型进行软变分训练；③基于泊松-二项（PB）分布进行最优层大小选择；④贝叶斯微调，最终得到稀疏、稳定、可解释且准确的模型。

Result: 在MNIST上仅保留8%的活跃节点；偶然-认知不确定性分解表明模型无知仅占总预测方差的3-4%；回归可靠性图显示预测区间覆盖率为93.4%（目标95%）。

Conclusion: NAP是一个可靠、有理论依据且计算可处理的解决方案，能够同时实现贝叶斯神经网络的稀疏性、准确性、可解释性和不确定性量化。

Original Abstract: Neural network compression and interpretability remain open challenges in modern deep learn- ing, where billion-parameter architectures deliver impressive accuracy at the cost of trans- parency, computational efficiency, and reliable uncertainty quantification. This paper introduces Neural Atom Prevalence (NAP), a principled Bayesian framework for structured node-level model selection in feedforward neural networks. NAP introduces the neural atom (activation unit) and functions as a hybrid method operating through a four-phase pipeline: Bayesian Lottery Ticket (BLT) identification via Iterative Magnitude Pruning (IMP), soft variational training of the Spike and Slab Independent Gaussian (SS-IG) model, Poisson-Binomial (PB) optimal layer-size selection, and Bayesian fine-tuning to produce a sparse, stable, interpretable, and accurate model. Extensive empirical validation across simulated nonlinear regression, two UCI benchmark datasets (Concrete, YearPredictionMSD), and the MNIST image classification task demonstrates that NAP achieves state-of-the-art structural sparsity, reducing active nodes to as few as 8% of the original dense architecture on MNIST, while well-calibrated probabilisti- cally: the aleatoric-epistemic uncertainty decomposition reveals that model ignorance accounts for only 3 to 4% of total predictive variance across all experiments, and regression reliability diagrams confirm a near-nominal predictive interval coverage (93.4% observed against a 95% target). These results establish NAP as a reliable, theoretically grounded, and computation- ally tractable solution to the simultaneous pursuit of sparsity, accuracy, interpretability, and uncertainty quantification in Bayesian neural networks.

中文摘要翻译: 神经网络压缩与可解释性仍然是现代深度学习中的开放挑战，数十亿参数的架构以牺牲透明性、计算效率和可靠的不确定性为代价实现了令人印象深刻的精度。本文提出神经原子盛行度（NAP），一个用于前馈神经网络中结构化节点级模型选择的原则性贝叶斯框架。NAP引入神经原子（激活单元），并通过四阶段流水线作为混合方法运作：通过迭代幅度剪枝（IMP）进行贝叶斯彩票（BLT）识别、Spike and Slab独立高斯（SS-IG）模型的软变分训练、基于泊松-二项（PB）的最优层大小选择，以及贝叶斯微调，以生成稀疏、稳定、可解释且准确的模型。在模拟非线性回归、两个UCI基准数据集（Concrete、YearPredictionMSD）以及MNIST图像分类任务上的广泛实证验证表明，NAP达到了最先进的结构稀疏性，在MNIST上将活跃节点减少至原始密集架构的8%，同时具有良好校准的概率特性：偶然-认知不确定性分解显示，在所有实验中，模型无知仅占总预测方差的3%至4%，回归可靠性图证实了接近名义水平的预测区间覆盖率（观测值93.4%，目标值95%）。这些结果确立了NAP作为同时追求贝叶斯神经网络稀疏性、准确性、可解释性和不确定性量化的一个可靠、有理论依据且计算可处理的解决方案。

</details>


### [89] [Self-Poisoning in Adaptive Out-of-Distribution Detection: A Sharp-Threshold Theory and Certified Label-Free Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21673)
*Vishnu Bindu Balachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文理论分析了测试时自适应OOD检测器的动态行为，证明其遵循广义Pólya瓮模型的几乎必然收敛规律，发现该检测器类天然处于近临界状态（斜率略低于1），并提出一种认证准入门控机制来切断反馈回路、消除相变，同时证明了无标签条件下漂移与污染不可区分的两世界不可能定理。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有测试时自适应OOD检测器通过未标记数据流更新记忆库，但其动态行为缺乏理论分析，尤其是在污染数据持续注入下记忆库何时崩溃尚不明确。本文旨在建立严格的理论框架来解释自适应OOD检测器的动力学规律，并设计可保证安全性的改进方案。

Method: 将记忆库杂质建模为广义Pólya瓮模型，证明其几乎必然收敛到平均场均衡；通过测量准入核的仿射性（R²≥0.996）揭示近临界特性；提出认证准入门控机制（仅读取冻结保留集）以切断反馈回路；针对静态校准失效提出CDC方法恢复标称FPR；最后证明漂移与污染无标签不可区分的两世界不可能定理。

Result: 理论证明杂质动态几乎必然收敛到平均场均衡，其斜率决定再生数；在96种设置中预测阈值与经验崩塌一致，无门控字典AUROC损失高达0.163；认证准入门控可在任意污染率（包括对抗性）下消除相变并控制假阳性；CDC方法在所有测试漂移单元上恢复标称FPR。

Conclusion: 建立了标签无关自适应OOD检测的完整可能性/不可能性刻画：认证准入门控可实现安全自适应检测，但无标签条件下漂移与污染本质上不可区分，存在封闭形式的功率上限。

Original Abstract: Test-time adaptive out-of-distribution (OOD) detectors update a memory bank from the unlabelled stream. We show this adaptation obeys a provable dynamical law. Modelling bank impurity as a generalized Pólya urn, we prove almost-sure convergence to a mean-field equilibrium whose slope acts as a reproduction number. Below one, impurity stays benign. Above one, the bank is fully poisoned and the detector collapses. The measured admission kernel is affine ($R^2 \ge 0.996$) with slope just below one in every encoder family (a protocol signature), so this detector class is near-critical by design, and across 96 settings the predicted threshold matches the empirical collapse, where ungated dictionaries lose up to $0.163$ AUROC. We then prove that a certified admission gate, reading only a frozen reserve, severs the feedback loop and removes the transition at every contamination rate, even adversarially, while controlling false positives label-free. For the complementary static-calibration failure under drift we give CDC, which restores nominal FPR label-free on all tested drift-affected cells. Finally we prove a two-world impossibility theorem. Drift and contamination are indistinguishable without labels, forcing a closed-form power ceiling our procedure approaches. Together these give a complete possibility/impossibility characterization of label-free adaptive OOD detection.

中文摘要翻译: 测试时自适应分布外（OOD）检测器会从未标记的数据流中更新记忆库。我们证明这种自适应遵循一个可证明的动力定律。将记忆库杂质建模为广义Pólya瓮模型，我们证明了其几乎必然收敛到平均场均衡，该均衡的斜率充当再生数。当斜率低于1时，杂质保持良性；高于1时，记忆库被完全污染，检测器崩溃。测得的准入核是仿射的（R² ≥ 0.996），在每个编码器族中斜率略低于1（一种协议签名），因此此类检测器在设计上处于近临界状态。在96种设置下，预测阈值与经验崩塌相匹配，其中无门控字典的AUROC损失高达0.163。我们进一步证明，一种认证准入门控机制——仅读取冻结的保留集——可以切断反馈回路并在任意污染率下（包括对抗性污染）消除相变，同时在无标签条件下控制假阳性。针对漂移下的互补性静态校准失败问题，我们提出了CDC方法，该方法能在所有受漂移影响的测试单元上无标签地恢复标称FPR。最后，我们证明了一个两世界不可能定理：在无标签条件下，漂移和污染是不可区分的，这迫使存在一个封闭形式的功率上限，而我们的方法逼近该上限。这些结果共同给出了标签无关自适应OOD检测的完整可能性/不可能性刻画。

</details>


### [90] [Encoding Invisible Causation for Bridge Diagnostic Agents: Triple-Guided Retrieval-Augmented Fine-Tuning with QLoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21680)
*Takato Yasuno*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种损伤原因编码器（Damage Cause Encoder），通过知识三元组提取、检索增强上下文和量化微调（QLoRA）三阶段流水线，实现了从可见损伤描述到10类损伤原因的分类，在保持87.07%准确率的同时显著降低了GPU内存消耗（降低72%）并提升了推理速度（11%），适用于边缘部署的自主桥梁诊断智能体。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 桥梁基础设施的退化是渐进的，但其根本原因（盐分侵入、冻融、疲劳开裂等）肉眼不可见，专家诊断依赖于长期积累的隐性知识。现有方法缺乏自动化这种潜在因果推理的能力，因此需要一种能在消费级硬件上高效运行的自主桥梁诊断方案。

Method: 提出三组件流水线：（i）知识三元组提取——使用大语言模型从15-35本诊断PDF手册中提取（损伤→原因）因果三元组，并索引至FAISS向量库；（ii）检索增强上下文——训练和推理时检索相关因果三元组并与损伤描述拼接，将隐性领域知识转化为显式编码器上下文；（iii）系统微调对比——在固定Golden Testset（116个分层样本）上对LoRA、QLoRA和QA-LoRA进行严格比较。

Result: QLoRA达到与全精度LoRA相同的测试准确率（87.07%），推理速度提升11%，GPU内存降低72%，且在覆盖全部10类损伤原因的100样本多样化评估中，QLoRA比LoRA高出13个百分点。

Conclusion: QLoRA在准确率、推理速度和内存效率之间实现了最优权衡，使得在消费级硬件上部署高精度、内存高效的桥梁诊断智能体成为可能，为边缘部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Bridge infrastructure deteriorates gradually, yet its root causes---salt intrusion, freezing, fatigue cracking, and others---remain invisible to the naked eye. Expert diagnosis relies on tacit knowledge built over years of practice. We address the challenge of automating this latent causal reasoning by proposing a Damage Cause Encoder that classifies 10-class damage causes from visible damage descriptions $S_i$ for use in autonomous bridge diagnostic agents. Our approach chains three components: (i)Knowledge Triple Extraction---a large language model extracts causal triples of the form (damage $\xrightarrow{\mathtt{caused\_by}}$ cause) from 15--35 diagnostic PDF manuals and indexes them in a FAISS vector store; (ii)Retrieval-Augmented Context---at training and inference time, relevant causal triples $\mathcal{C}_i$ are retrieved and concatenated with $S_i$, converting implicit domain knowledge into explicit Encoder context; (iii)Systematic Fine-tuning Comparison---we conduct a rigorous comparison of LoRA, QLoRA, and QA-LoRA on a fixed Golden Testset (116 stratified samples), demonstrating that QLoRA achieves the optimal trade-off: identical test accuracy (87.07%) to full-precision LoRA, 11% faster inference, 72% lower GPU memory, and superior generalization across diverse unseen inputs. A controlled Golden Testset---stratified, deduplicated, and difficulty-tagged---is introduced as a reusable benchmark contribution. QLoRA further outperforms LoRA by 13 percentage points on a 100-sample diverse evaluation spanning all 10 damage cause classes.These findings enable memory-efficient, high-accuracy diagnostic agents on consumer-grade hardware for edge deployment.

中文摘要翻译: 桥梁基础设施会逐渐退化，但其根本原因——盐分侵入、冻融、疲劳开裂等——肉眼无法直接观测。专家诊断依赖于多年实践积累的隐性知识。本文旨在解决自动化这种潜在因果推理的挑战，提出了一种损伤原因编码器（Damage Cause Encoder），可从可见的损伤描述S_i中分类出10类损伤原因，用于自主桥梁诊断智能体。我们的方法由三个组件串联而成：（i）知识三元组提取——使用大语言模型从15-35本诊断PDF手册中提取（损伤 → 原因）形式的因果三元组，并将其索引到FAISS向量存储中；（ii）检索增强上下文——在训练和推理阶段，检索相关的因果三元组C_i并与S_i拼接，将隐性的领域知识转化为显式的编码器上下文；（iii）系统微调对比——我们在固定的Golden Testset（116个分层样本）上对LoRA、QLoRA和QA-LoRA进行了严格比较，结果表明QLoRA实现了最优的权衡：与全精度LoRA相比，测试准确率相同（87.07%），推理速度提升11%，GPU内存降低72%，且在多样的未见输入上具有更优的泛化能力。本文还引入了一个经过分层、去重和难度标记的可控Golden Testset，作为可复用的基准贡献。在覆盖全部10类损伤原因的100样本多样化评估中，QLoRA相比LoRA高出13个百分点。这些发现使得在消费级硬件上部署内存高效、高精度的诊断智能体成为可能，适用于边缘部署场景。

</details>


### [91] [CARNet Cycle-Conditioned Core Aggregation and Redistribution for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21681)
*Awsaf Tausif Adib,Md. Shahria Sarker Shuvo,Md. Estehaar Ahmed Emon,Mustafa Kamal,Fuad Rahman,Shafin Rahman,Nabeel Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARNet提出了一种基于周期条件核聚合与重分配的多元时间序列预测框架，通过将全局循环周期信息融入线性复杂度的核交互建模，在多个基准上优于Transformer和非注意力基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有注意力机制方法存在二次复杂度问题，随变量数量增加扩展性差；而无注意力聚合模型虽为线性复杂度，但未能充分利用数据中的全局周期结构信息。

Method: 提出CARNet框架，通过多头核聚合将全局循环周期信息融入高效的基于核的交互建模中，实现周期条件核聚合与重分配。

Result: 在多个真实世界的多元时间序列预测基准上，CARNet在不同预测长度下一致优于强Transformer和非注意力基线方法。

Conclusion: CARNet能够在保持跨变量依赖关系线性复杂度建模的同时，有效利用全局周期结构信息，显著提升多元时间序列预测性能。

Original Abstract: Accurately modeling cross-variate dependencies remains a key challenge in multivariate time series forecasting, particularly in the presence of strong periodic patterns. Many existing approaches rely on attention-based mechanisms that incur quadratic complexity and scale poorly with increasing numbers of variates. Recent attention-free aggregation models address this issue through linear-complexity core-based interactions, but they do not explicitly leverage the global periodic structure present in the data. To overcome this limitation, we propose CARNet, a Cycle-Conditioned Core Aggregation and Redistribution framework that integrates global recurrent cycle information into efficient core based interaction modeling via Multihead Core Aggregation. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world multivariate forecasting benchmarks demonstrate that CARNet consistently outperforms strong transformer and non-attention baselines across diverse prediction horizons while preserving linear-complexity modeling of cross-variate dependencies.

中文摘要翻译: 准确建模跨变量依赖关系仍然是多元时间序列预测中的一个关键挑战，尤其是在存在强周期模式的情况下。许多现有方法依赖基于注意力的机制，这些机制具有二次复杂度，并且随着变量数量的增加而扩展性较差。近期无注意力聚合模型通过线性复杂度的核交互解决了这一问题，但它们并未显式利用数据中存在的全局周期结构。为克服这一局限，我们提出CARNet——一个周期条件核聚合与重分配框架，通过多头核聚合将全局循环周期信息融入高效的基于核的交互建模中。在多个真实世界多元预测基准上的大量实验表明，CARNet在保持跨变量依赖关系线性复杂度建模的同时，在不同预测长度上一致优于强Transformer和无注意力基线方法。

</details>


### [92] [Learning What Matters: Supervising Sparse Attention Routing with Causal Evidence Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21692)
*Jim Allchin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现注意力机制（attention）与因果依赖（causal dependence）在检索任务中经常不一致，基于注意力蒸馏训练的稀疏选择器会继承这种偏差；而基于因果证据（通过掩码实验获得）训练的相同选择器能匹配教师模型性能，无需额外标注。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力训练通常通过蒸馏密集教师模型的注意力模式来训练选择器，隐式假设注意力能够揭示模型实际使用的上下文。论文旨在检验这一假设是否成立，即注意力是否真正反映了模型决策所依赖的证据。

Method: 通过在检索任务中对输入上下文进行部分掩码，观察答案是否变化，从而测量因果依赖关系（与注意力模式对比）；在两步引用任务中，分别用注意力模式和因果证据训练选择器并对比性能；在预训练模型（Qwen2.5-3B, Gemma-2-9B）上分析矛盾事实场景下的注意力与因果依赖关系。

Result: 1) 注意力与因果依赖经常不一致，蒸馏选择器继承这一不匹配；2) 教师模型会关注已学会忽略的过时事实，且注意力在不同训练运行中会变化；3) 两步引用任务中，注意力训练的选择器准确率41%，因果证据训练的达到99%；4) 因果证据集无需人工标注，仅通过掩码即可从冻结教师中恢复；5) Qwen2.5-3B在58%的矛盾事实例子中更多关注过时事实却回答正确，Gemma-2-9B在限制为相关句子时准确率从56%升至99%。

Conclusion: 注意力展示的是模型"看向哪里"，而非其答案"依赖什么"；因果依赖作为训练目标在各方面均匹配或优于注意力模式。

Original Abstract: Sparse attention reduces the cost of long contexts by allowing each query to read only selected parts of the input. These selectors are often trained by distilling the attention patterns of a dense teacher, assuming that attention reveals which context the teacher actually uses. We test that assumption on retrieval tasks where the evidence for each answer is known exactly. By masking parts of the context and measuring whether the answer changes, we find that attention and causal dependence often disagree, and distilled selectors inherit the mismatch. Teachers attend to outdated facts they have learned to ignore, and their attention can vary across training runs even when they rely on the same evidence. In a two-step reference task, attention at the answer skips the intermediate step because it was resolved earlier in the forward pass: a selector trained on attention achieves 41% accuracy, while the same selector trained on causal evidence reaches 99% and matches the teacher. These evidence sets require no annotation: recovered from a frozen teacher by masking alone, they train selectors to the same accuracy. We find the same conflict in pretrained models: Qwen2.5-3B gives more attention to an outdated fact than the current one on 58% of conflicting-fact examples despite answering correctly, while Gemma-2-9B rises from 56% to 99% accuracy when restricted to the two relevant sentences. Attention shows where a model looks, not necessarily what its answer depends on; across the regimes we tested, that dependence matched or outperformed attention as a training target.

中文摘要翻译: 稀疏注意力通过允许每个查询只读取输入的选定部分来降低长上下文的计算成本。这些选择器通常通过蒸馏密集教师模型的注意力模式来训练，其隐含假设是注意力能揭示教师实际使用的上下文。我们在检索任务上检验了这一假设，其中每个答案的证据是确切已知的。通过掩码上下文的部分内容并测量答案是否改变，我们发现注意力与因果依赖经常不一致，并且蒸馏选择器继承了这一不匹配。教师模型会关注它们已学会忽略的过时事实，并且即使依赖相同证据，其注意力在不同训练运行之间也会变化。在一个两步引用任务中，答案位置的注意力跳过了中间步骤，因为它在前向传递中已被更早地解析：基于注意力训练的选择器准确率为41%，而基于因果证据训练的相同选择器达到99%并匹配教师模型性能。这些证据集不需要任何标注：仅通过掩码即可从冻结的教师模型中恢复，它们训练的选择器达到相同的准确率。我们在预训练模型中也发现了同样的矛盾：Qwen2.5-3B在58%的矛盾事实例子中更多关注过时事实而非当前事实，尽管回答正确；而Gemma-2-9B在限制为两个相关句子时准确率从56%上升到99%。注意力展示的是模型"看向哪里"，而不一定是其答案"依赖什么"；在我们测试的所有场景中，因果依赖作为训练目标匹配或优于注意力模式。

</details>


### [93] [An Introduction to Bayesian and Frequentist Simulation-Based Inference with Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21702)
*Maximilian Dax,Theo Heimel,Gilles Louppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于机器学习的仿真推断（SBI）方法在科学和工程逆问题中的应用，涵盖贝叶斯与频率学派统计框架下的神经后验估计和神经似然估计，并扩展到经验贝叶斯与解折叠任务，同时讨论了验证策略与局限性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 为科学和工程中的逆问题（参数推断、探测器效应反演）提供基于机器学习的仿真推断方法的系统性概述，帮助研究者理解不同统计框架下的方法选择与应用。

Method: 综述了贝叶斯与频率学派统计框架下基于机器学习的SBI方法，包括神经后验估计（NPE）和神经似然估计（NLE），并将其应用于经验贝叶斯与解折叠（unfolding）任务。

Result: 展示了NPE和NLE等基于机器学习的SBI方法可在贝叶斯和频率学派框架下进行参数估计，并能推广到经验贝叶斯及解折叠任务中。

Conclusion: 基于机器学习的仿真推断是解决逆问题的有力工具，但需要谨慎验证推断结果并注意其局限性。

Original Abstract: Simulation-based inference (SBI) with machine learning is an increasingly important tool for solving inverse problems in science and engineering, including parameter inference and the inversion of detector effects. We provide an overview of the Bayesian and frequentist statistical frameworks, describe how machine-learning-based SBI methods, such as neural posterior estimation and neural likelihood estimation, can be used for parameter estimation within these frameworks, and show that the same methods can also be applied to Empirical Bayes or unfolding tasks. We also discuss how to validate inference results and the limitations of SBI with machine learning.

中文摘要翻译: 基于机器学习的仿真推断（SBI）正成为解决科学和工程中逆问题（包括参数推断和探测器效应反演）日益重要的工具。我们概述了贝叶斯和频率学派统计框架，描述了如何在这些框架内使用基于机器学习的SBI方法（如神经后验估计和神经似然估计）进行参数估计，并展示了相同的方法也可应用于经验贝叶斯或解折叠任务。我们还讨论了如何验证推断结果以及基于机器学习的SBI的局限性。

</details>


### [94] [A Defense of the Quadratic Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21716)
*Alexandru Meterez,Pranav Ajit Nair,Depen Morwani,Cengiz Pehlevan,Sham Kakade,Alex Damian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文表明，在大型语言模型（1.5亿参数，30亿token）预训练中，最简单的二次优化模型能惊人准确地预测长达10%训练窗口内的优化动态，并通过Hessian谱分析和局部线性稳定性分析揭示了批大小、预条件和训练时间对优化结构的影响，指出LLM优化通常处于随机边缘稳定性状态。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 神经网络的损失景观极为复杂，优化理论被迫依赖理想化模型，且模型的理论可解性与对真实优化动态的描述精度之间存在权衡。作者希望通过压力测试最简单的二次优化模型，检验其在LLM大规模预训练中的预测能力。

Method: 1) 在中间检查点对模型和损失函数进行泰勒展开，构建局部二次模型来预测后续优化动态；2) 使用Lanczos求积法配合极深探针，估计Hessian谱的尾部结构；3) 通过检查点的局部线性稳定性分析，比较理论预测与实验结果。

Result: 1) 二次模型可在长达10%训练时间的窗口内准确预测优化动态；2) Hessian谱在特征值和特征向量层面均表现出显著的结构性，且依赖于批大小、预条件和训练时间；3) LLM优化通常处于随机边缘稳定性状态，其性质由批大小决定。

Conclusion: 二次优化模型可以作为预训练优化动态的一个理论可解的有效代理，为理解大规模LLM训练的优化行为提供了简化但有力的分析框架。

Original Abstract: Due to the complexity of neural network loss landscapes, optimization theory is forced to rely on idealized models, and there is generally a tradeoff between how theoretically tractable the model is, and how accurately it describes the true optimization dynamics. In this work, we stress test the simplest possible model of optimization -- the quadratic model -- and show that it can be surprisingly predictive in an LLM setting with 150M parameters and 3B training tokens. Specifically, we show that Taylor expanding the model and the loss function at intermediate checkpoints through training can accurately predict the optimization dynamics over windows that can last up to 10\% of training. Having established this agreement, we then turn to analyzing the structure of these local quadratic optimization problems through two lenses: the Hessian spectrum and local stability. Using Lanczos quadrature with extremely deep probes, we are able to estimate the Hessian spectrum deep into the tail, and we find a surprising amount of structure in both the eigenvalues and eigenvectors, which depends on the batch size, preconditioner, and training time. We also empirically test local linear stability at intermediate checkpoints and compare it to theoretical predictions to demonstrate that optimization in LLMs typically occurs at a stochastic edge of stability, whose nature is also determined by batch size. Our results indicate the quadratic model may be a theoretically tractable proxy for pretraining optimization dynamics.

中文摘要翻译: 由于神经网络损失景观的复杂性，优化理论被迫依赖理想化模型，且模型的理论可解性与对真实优化动态的描述精度之间存在普遍权衡。在这项工作中，我们对最简单的优化模型——二次模型——进行了压力测试，并证明它在1.5亿参数和30亿训练tokens的LLM场景中具有惊人的预测能力。具体而言，我们展示了在训练过程中的中间检查点对模型和损失函数进行泰勒展开，能够准确预测长达训练10%时间窗口内的优化动态。在确立这一一致性后，我们转而通过两个视角——Hessian谱和局部稳定性——来分析这些局部二次优化问题的结构。利用具有极深探针的Lanczos求积法，我们能够估计Hessian谱深入尾部的分布，并在特征值和特征向量中都发现了惊人的结构性，这种结构依赖于批大小、预条件和训练时间。我们还通过实验检验了中间检查点的局部线性稳定性，并与理论预测进行比较，证明LLM中的优化通常发生在一种随机边缘稳定性状态，其性质同样由批大小决定。我们的结果表明，二次模型可能是一个理论可解的预训练优化动态代理。

</details>


### [95] [Remedying Coarsening-Based GNN Training under Heterophily via Adaptive Complementary Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21885)
*Guoming Li,Jian Yang,Xukun Wang,Zixiao Wang,Shangsong Liang,Yifan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对基于粗化训练的图神经网络在同配图和异配图上表现差异显著的问题，提出一种即插即用的自适应互补增强策略ACE，通过重构原始特征和各向异性结构正则化来恢复粗化过程中丢失的信息，并利用同方差不确定性加权平衡训练目标，显著提升异配图性能的同时保持同配图的竞争力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于粗化训练的GNN方法几乎仅在**同配图**上被评估，在更具挑战性的**异配图**上表现严重下降。作者通过理论和实验证明，粗化过程不可避免地丢失图信息，导致在异配图上性能显著退化，亟需一种能恢复丢失信息的方法。

Method: 提出**ACE**（自适应互补增强）策略，包含三个核心组件：(1) 学习投影器重构原始节点特征，恢复粗化中丢弃的信息；(2) **各向异性结构正则化**，嵌入局部异配性；(3) **同方差不确定性加权**，自适应平衡粗化图主损失与全图辅助损失（使用异配感知投影器重构增强节点特征）。ACE是即插即用、模型无关的策略。

Result: 大量实验表明，ACE在异配图基准上取得**一致且显著的性能提升**，同时在同配图上保持**有竞争力的结果**，且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: ACE有效解决了粗化训练在异配图上的性能退化问题，通过恢复丢失信息实现跨同配/异配图的鲁棒性能，是一种高效且通用的GNN训练增强策略。

Original Abstract: Coarsening-based training for graph neural networks (GNNs), i.e.\ training on coarsened graphs rather than the original large ones, has become a promising direction for scaling GNNs to massive graphs. However, prior work has been evaluated almost exclusively on \textit{homophilic} graphs, leaving the more challenging \textit{heterophilic} settings underexplored. We show, both empirically and theoretically, that existing coarsening-based training methods suffer significant performance degradation on heterophilic graphs due to inevitable loss of graph information during coarsening. To address this, we propose {\bf A}daptive {\bf C}omplementary {\bf E}nhancement, a plug-and-play, model-agnostic strategy that reintegrates the information discarded in coarsening: ACE learns a projector for re-constructing original node features and applies \textit{anisotropic structural regularization} to embed local heterophily. We further adopt \textit{homoscedastic uncertainty weighting} to adaptively balance the combined training objective of primary coarsened-graph training loss and full-graph auxiliary loss with augmented node features re-constructed by the heterophily-aware projector. Extensive experiments show that ACE drives consistent gains on heterophilic benchmarks while preserving competitive results on homophilic graphs with minimal computational overhead. Code is available at the GitHub repository: https://github.com/vasile-paskardlgm/ACE.

中文摘要翻译: 基于粗化训练的图神经网络（GNN），即在粗化图而非原始大图上训练，已成为将GNN扩展至大规模图的有前景方向。然而，先前的工作几乎仅在**同配图**上进行评估，使得更具挑战性的**异配**设置未被充分探索。我们通过实证和理论表明，由于粗化过程中不可避免的图信息损失，现有基于粗化训练的方法在异配图上遭受显著的性能退化。为解决此问题，我们提出**自适应互补增强（ACE）**，一种即插即用、模型无关的策略，用于重新整合粗化过程中丢弃的信息：ACE学习一个投影器以重构原始节点特征，并应用**各向异性结构正则化**来嵌入局部异配性。我们进一步采用**同方差不确定性加权**来自适应平衡联合训练目标，即主粗化图训练损失和具有异配感知投影器重构的增强节点特征的全图辅助损失。大量实验表明，ACE在异配图基准上实现一致增益，同时在同配图上以极小的计算开销保持有竞争力的结果。代码已在GitHub仓库中提供：https://github.com/vasile-paskardlgm/ACE。

</details>


### [96] [RED-PIM: Reducing Data Movement for Transformers using Processing-in-Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21731)
*Zahra Yousefijamarani,Alaa Alameldeen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RED-PIM通过算法-架构协同设计，将注意力机制中的跨bank数据移动从O(N²)减少到O(N)，并将中间注意力矩阵从N×N压缩到d×d，从而显著加速Transformer推理，相比基线PIM实现平均加速66.42%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Transformer模型广泛使用，但注意力操作中处理单元与内存之间的数据移动严重制约了效率。现有基于PIM的实现存在昂贵的跨bank通信问题，且受限于内存bank容量难以扩展，注意力数据被迫拆分到多个bank中，削弱了PIM的优势。

Method: 提出RED-PIM算法-架构协同设计方案：(1) 通过重组矩阵运算将跨bank数据移动从O(N²)降至O(N)；(2) 将中间注意力矩阵从N×N缩小为d×d；(3) 在本地执行计算；(4) 采用优化的数据传输策略，从而显著降低计算开销和互连流量。

Result: 相比基线PIM实现，RED-PIM推理时间降低16.05%~99.99%（几何均值66.42%），长序列收益最大。在真实数据集上，长文档性能提升99.60%，短文档提升13.44%，同时保持或提升了模型精度。

Conclusion: RED-PIM实现了可扩展且高效的Transformer推理，证明了算法-架构协同设计在解决PIM注意力计算瓶颈方面的有效性。

Original Abstract: Transformers are widely used across many domains, including natural language processing, computer vision, web search, and DNA sequence analysis. Given their broad applicability, improving the performance of transformer models is critical. However, the high volume of data movement between processing units and memory during attention operations significantly limits their efficiency. Processing-In-Memory (PIM) mitigates this issue by performing computations directly inside memory. While prior work has proposed PIM-based transformer implementations, they suffer from costly inter-bank communication, and struggle to scale due to the limited capacity of memory banks. As a result, attention-related data must be split across banks, diminishing the potential benefits of PIM.
  In this work, we propose RED-PIM, an algorithm-architecture co-design that reduces attention latency by minimizing inter-bank data movement from O(N^2) to O(N) and shrinking intermediate attention matrices from N x N to d x d. By reorganizing matrix operations, performing computations locally, and employing an optimized data transfer strategy, RED-PIM significantly reduces computation cost and interconnect traffic. Compared to baseline PIM implementation, RED-PIM achieves inference time reductions ranging from 16.05% to 99.99% (geometric mean of 66.42%), with the largest gains on longer sequences. On real-world datasets, RED-PIM improves performance by 99.60% for long documents and 13.44% for shorter ones, while maintaining or improving accuracy. These results demonstrate RED-PIM's effectiveness for scalable and efficient transformer inference.

中文摘要翻译: Transformer广泛应用于自然语言处理、计算机视觉、网络搜索和DNA序列分析等多个领域。鉴于其广泛的适用性，提升Transformer模型的性能至关重要。然而，注意力操作期间处理单元与内存之间的大量数据移动严重限制了其效率。处理中内存（PIM）技术通过直接在内存内部执行计算来缓解这一问题。尽管已有工作提出了基于PIM的Transformer实现，但它们存在昂贵的跨bank通信开销，并且由于内存bank容量有限而难以扩展。因此，注意力相关数据必须跨bank拆分，从而削弱了PIM的潜在优势。在本工作中，我们提出RED-PIM，一种算法-架构协同设计方案，通过将跨bank数据移动从O(N²)减少到O(N)，并将中间注意力矩阵从N×N缩小到d×d，从而降低注意力延迟。通过重组矩阵运算、在本地执行计算以及采用优化的数据传输策略，RED-PIM显著降低了计算成本和互连流量。与基线PIM实现相比，RED-PIM实现了16.05%至99.99%的推理时间减少（几何均值66.42%），在更长序列上收益最大。在真实世界数据集上，RED-PIM对长文档的性能提升达99.60%，对较短文档提升13.44%，同时保持或提升了精度。这些结果证明了RED-PIM在可扩展且高效的Transformer推理中的有效性。

</details>


### [97] [Parameter-free Adaptive Sparse Attention via Compression-Based Content Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21752)
*Debarshi Kundu,Swaroop Ghosh,Vasant Honavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出利用gzip压缩比作为自适应稀疏注意力掩码的信号，无需额外参数，在长序列语言建模中显著优于固定掩码和现有自适应方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自适应稀疏注意力方法（如SBM-Transformer、Dynamic Mask Attention、NSA）虽优于固定模式，但都需要额外学习参数、自定义梯度估计或专用CUDA核。本文旨在探索一种无需任何额外参数的纯数据驱动方法。

Method: 计算每个block的gzip压缩比，压缩比高的block（难以压缩）包含更多非冗余信息，被视为长距离注意力的天然目标。通过这种输入依赖的压缩特征图，动态生成稀疏注意力掩码，无需学习参数、辅助损失或自定义核。

Result: 在PG-19字节级语言建模（92M参数，8K上下文）上达到1.71 BPB，优于密集注意力（2.89）、BigBird（2.34）、Longformer（3.21）和SBM-Transformer（3.38），最大提升1.67 BPB。优势随序列长度增加而扩大，收敛速度提升3.3倍。

Conclusion: 经典数据压缩（gzip）能有效提供自适应注意力掩码信号，无需任何额外参数，在长序列建模中性能显著且效率更高。

Original Abstract: Data-adaptive sparse attention masks substantially outperform fixed patterns (e.g., BigBird and Longformer) and can even exceed dense attention on long sequences. Existing adaptive approaches---including SBM-Transformer, Dynamic Mask Attention, and NSA---typically require additional learnable parameters, custom gradient estimators, or specialized CUDA kernels.
  We show that classical data compression provides an effective masking signal with \textbf{no additional parameters}. By computing per-block gzip compression ratios, we identify non-redundant content blocks and route long-range attention selectively through them. Intuitively, blocks that gzip cannot compress contain information not predictable from local repetition, making them natural long-range attention targets. Because the compression profile is input-dependent, the resulting sparse mask adapts dynamically to content without learned parameters, auxiliary losses, or custom kernels.
  On PG-19 byte-level language modeling at 92M parameters with 8K context, our method achieves 1.71 bits-per-byte (BPB), outperforming dense attention (2.89), BigBird (2.34), Longformer (3.21), and a reimplemented SBM-Transformer (3.38)---the only learned-mask baseline---by up to 1.67 BPB while adding no parameters. The advantage grows with sequence length, with the gap over BigBird widening from 0.05 BPB at 4K context to 0.63 BPB at 8K, while convergence is 3.3$\times$ faster.

中文摘要翻译: 数据自适应的稀疏注意力掩码大幅优于固定模式（如BigBird和Longformer），甚至在长序列上可以超越密集注意力。现有的自适应方法——包括SBM-Transformer、Dynamic Mask Attention和NSA——通常需要额外的可学习参数、自定义梯度估计器或专用CUDA核。本文证明，经典数据压缩能够提供有效的掩码信号，且无需任何额外参数。通过计算每个块的gzip压缩比，我们识别出非冗余的内容块，并选择性地将长距离注意力路由到这些块上。直观上，gzip无法压缩的块包含无法通过局部重复预测的信息，使其成为天然的长距离注意力目标。由于压缩特征是输入依赖的，由此产生的稀疏掩码能够根据内容自适应地动态调整，无需学习参数、辅助损失或自定义核。在PG-19字节级语言建模任务上（92M参数，8K上下文），我们的方法实现了1.71 bits-per-byte（BPB），优于密集注意力（2.89）、BigBird（2.34）、Longformer（3.21）以及重新实现的SBM-Transformer（3.38）——其中SBM-Transformer是唯一的学习掩码基线——最高提升了1.67 BPB，且未增加任何参数。该优势随序列长度增长而扩大，在4K上下文到8K上下文范围内，与BigBird的差距从0.05 BPB扩大到0.63 BPB，同时收敛速度提升了3.3倍。

</details>


### [98] [Interpretable EEG biomarkers with bag-of-waves: Spatial and temporal waveform dictionaries for low-data regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22508)
*Athanasios Papastathopoulos-Katsaros,Steven T. Lee,Lin Yao,Ajay Thomas,Junseok Park,Matthew J. McGinley,Zhandong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出bag-of-waves框架，通过无监督的移位不变k均值学习少量可解释的EEG波形模板（原子），将连续EEG转化为原子令牌序列，结合n-gram捕捉时序结构和多通道空间原子，在三个数据集上以极低参数量达到与深度/基础模型相当的性能，尤其适合小数据场景。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有EEG分析方法存在两难：预定义频谱特征带有强偏置且信息固定，深度神经网络和基础模型虽强但难以解释且需要大量数据和计算资源。本文旨在开发一种既具有可解释性又能在小数据场景下高效工作的EEG分析方法。

Method: 提出bag-of-waves框架：1）使用移位不变k均值无监督学习少量可复现的EEG波形模板（原子）；2）将连续EEG信号转化为原子令牌序列，通过原子计数进行下游分类/聚类；3）扩展至原子间转移（n-gram）捕获时序结构；4）扩展至区域性和跨通道空间原子处理多通道情况。

Result: 在三个互补数据集上验证：1）单通道小鼠基因型聚类（仅16只动物）表现优异；2）静息态痴呆分类任务表现良好；3）TUEV基准六分类临床EEG事件分类中，以极低参数量达到与最先进深度和基础模型竞争的性能。方法完全可解释，每个原子对应可检查的波形，能恢复已知临床形态学特征。

Conclusion: bag-of-waves框架在保持完全可解释性的同时，以极低参数量达到与深度和基础模型竞争的性能，其主要优势在于小数据场景下表现优异，解决了现有方法在可解释性和数据效率之间的权衡问题。

Original Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is widely used to diagnose neurological conditions, but its analysis usually relies on either predefined spectral features or deep neural networks. Predefined features carry a strong bias, since they fix in advance what counts as informative, while deep neural networks and foundation models are hard to interpret and need large amounts of data and compute. We present bag-of-waves, an interpretable framework that learns a small dictionary of recurring EEG waveform templates, called atoms, using shift-invariant k-means without labels. The continuous EEG is then turned into a sequence of atom tokens, whose counts feed a simple downstream classifier or clustering step. We extend this representation in two ways: we add atom-to-atom transitions, which we call n- grams, to capture temporal structure, and we move from single-channel atoms to regional and cross-channel spatial atoms for the multichannel case. We test the method on three complementary datasets, each probing a different aspect: single-channel mouse genotype clustering with only sixteen animals (the low-data and temporal case), resting-state dementia classification (the spatial case), and the TUEV benchmark, a six-way classification of clinical EEG events (a high-data comparison against strong deep and foundation baselines). Across all three datasets, bag-of-waves achieves performance competitive with state-of-the-art deep and foundation models. Yet, it operates with a fraction of the parameter count and provides full interpretability: because every atom corresponds to an inspectable waveform, the method explicitly recovers known clinical morphologies that a neurophysiologist can directly validate. Its main advantage is that it works in the low-data regime where heavier models are a poor fit.

中文摘要翻译: 脑电图（EEG）广泛用于诊断神经系统疾病，但其分析通常依赖于预定义的频谱特征或深度神经网络。预定义特征带有强烈的偏置，因为它们预先固定了哪些信息是有用的，而深度神经网络和基础模型难以解释，且需要大量数据和计算资源。我们提出bag-of-waves，一个可解释的框架，它使用无标签的移位不变k均值学习一个小的可复现EEG波形模板字典，称为原子。连续的EEG信号被转化为原子令牌序列，其计数结果供简单的下游分类器或聚类步骤使用。我们以两种方式扩展这种表示：引入原子到原子之间的转移（称为n-gram）以捕获时序结构，并从单通道原子扩展到区域性和跨通道的空间原子以处理多通道情况。我们在三个互补的数据集上测试该方法，每个数据集侧重于不同方面：仅16只动物的单通道小鼠基因型聚类（低数据与时序场景）、静息态痴呆分类（空间场景），以及TUEV基准——一个六分类临床EEG事件分类任务（与强深度和基础模型对比的高数据场景）。在全部三个数据集上，bag-of-waves均达到了与最先进的深度和基础模型相竞争的性能。然而，其参数量仅为这些模型的一小部分，并提供了完全的可解释性：由于每个原子对应一个可检查的波形，该方法能够显式地恢复已知的临床形态学特征，神经生理学家可以直接验证这些特征。其主要优势在于在较重模型不适用的低数据场景下仍能良好工作。

</details>


### [99] [Smart predict-then-robustly-optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21773)
*Aakil Caunhye,Xuefei Lu,Belen Martin-Barragan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种鲁棒的“预测-然后优化”变体，在协变量特征空间存在扰动时仍能保持决策性能，通过将鲁棒优化直接集成到预测-决策管线中，并建立了计算可处理的凸代理函数，理论证明了其近似误差指数衰减和Fisher一致性，实验表明该方法在样本外性能和训练稳定性上均优于传统方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统集成学习与优化模型假设特征信息完全准确，但实际数据驱动的特征在决策时常被噪声污染或损坏，导致策略脆弱。为弥合这一差距，需要一种能对抗最坏情况特征扰动的鲁棒预测-优化方法。

Method: 提出了一种“智能预测-然后鲁棒优化”损失函数，将鲁棒优化原则直接集成到预测-决策管线中，并建立了一个计算可处理的凸代理函数，以对冲最坏情况的特征扰动。

Result: 理论上证明了代理函数的近似误差概率以次高斯浓度分布指数衰减，并在温和假设下证明该代理函数具有高概率的Fisher一致性；还证明了该方法优于标准智能预测-然后优化方法的必要条件。数值实验验证了该方法在样本外性能和训练稳定性上均显著优于标准方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的鲁棒预测-然后优化框架能有效应对特征空间的扰动，在理论上具有良好的统计性质，在实践中相比传统方法具有显著的性能优势。

Original Abstract: In this paper, we propose and study a robust variant of the smart predict-then-optimize approach that accounts for prediction shifts due to disturbance in the covariate feature space. While traditional integrated-learning-and-optimization models assume that side information is perfectly revealed, empirical data-driven features are frequently corrupted or noisy at the time of decision-making, leading to fragile operational policies. To bridge this gap, we integrate principles of robust optimization directly into the predictive-prescriptive pipeline via a smart predict-then-robustly optimize loss and establish a computationally tractable convex surrogate, designed to hedge against worst-case feature perturbations. On the theoretical front, we formalize the structural validity of this surrogate by proving its approximation error probability decays exponentially according to a sub-Gaussian concentration profile. Furthermore, we establish that under mild assumptions, the surrogate is Fisher consistent with high probability. We also prove necessary conditions under which our framework outperforms standard smart predict-then-optimize and maintain its superiority even when the standard method is equipped with regularized upstream predictions. Numerical experiments validate that our robust framework consistently yields significant performance improvements over standard methods, both in out-of-sample terms and in training stability.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出并研究了一种鲁棒的智能预测-然后优化方法变体，用于应对协变量特征空间中因扰动导致的预测偏移。传统的集成学习与优化模型假设辅助信息完美揭示，但经验数据驱动的特征在决策时常被破坏或噪声污染，导致运营策略脆弱。为弥合这一差距，我们通过一种智能预测-然后鲁棒优化损失将鲁棒优化原则直接集成到预测-决策管线中，并建立了一个计算可处理的凸代理函数，用于对冲最坏情况的特征扰动。在理论层面，我们通过证明该代理函数的近似误差概率以次高斯浓度分布指数衰减，形式化地验证了其结构有效性。此外，我们证明了在温和假设下该代理函数具有高概率的Fisher一致性。我们还证明了该框架优于标准智能预测-然后优化方法的必要条件，并且即使标准方法配备了正则化的上游预测，本框架仍能保持其优越性。数值实验验证了我们的鲁棒框架在样本外性能和训练稳定性方面均显著优于标准方法。

</details>


### [100] [Physiological Signals as a Forensic Modality for Talking-Face Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21776)
*Othmane Harraq,Tamer Aldwairi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于rPPG生理信号的说话人脸深度伪造检测框架，利用RhythmFormer提取波形并结合轻量级分类器进行鉴别。在Celeb-DF++的TF子集上，1D ResNet达到AUC 0.806，并揭示了不同生成方法的检测难度差异源于可解释的生理特性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像级检测器对说话人脸(TF)深度伪造视频识别效果差；TF合成没有真实视频作为底本，无法继承生理特征，因此远程光电容积描记法(rPPG)成为独特的检测模态。

Method: 通过RhythmFormer从每段视频中提取rPPG波形，训练一组轻量级二分类器（如1D ResNet）区分真实与合成的生理信号。

Result: 在Celeb-DF++的TF子集上，严格身份无关协议下1D ResNet AUC为0.806、EER为27.8%；复现DeepFakesON-Phys发现其在TF数据上从AUC 0.999（传统换脸）降至0.622；七个TF生成器的AUC从0.985到0.690不等，排名在所有评估协议下完全稳定。

Conclusion: rPPG生理信号是检测TF深度伪造的有效单一模态；检测难度差异源于各生成器的可解释生理特性，而非评估噪声，构成主要理论贡献。

Original Abstract: Talking-face (TF) deepfake generation synthesizes photore- alistic facial video from a static source image and an au- dio signal, producing forgeries that current image-based detectors consistently fail to identify. Unlike face-swap ma- nipulation, TF synthesis has no underlying real video from which to inherit physiological characteristics, making re- mote photoplethysmography (rPPG) a uniquely motivated detection modality for this forgery category. We propose a detection framework that extracts per-video rPPG wave- forms via RhythmFormer and trains a suite of lightweight classifiers to distinguish real from synthesized physiologi- cal signals. Evaluated on the TF subset of Celeb-DF++ un- der a strict subject-independent protocol, where test identi- ties are completely separated from training identities, our 1D ResNet achieves an AUC of 0.806 and EER of 27.8%, placing it within 2.4 points of the best published general- purpose detector (Effort, ICML 2025) while operating ex- clusively on the physiological channel. We document a con- trolled reproduction study of DeepFakesON-Phys, the rep- resentative prior rPPG detector, demonstrating degrada- tion from AUC 0.999 on legacy face-swap data to 0.622 on the TF subset of Celeb-DF++. We further show that detec- tion difficulty is strongly method-dependent: AUC ranges from 0.985 (Real3DPortrait) to 0.690 (IP-LAP) across the seven TF generators, with the ranking remaining perfectly stable across all evaluation protocols. This spread reflects an interpretable physiological property of each generator rather than evaluation noise, and constitutes the primary theoretical contribution of the work.

中文摘要翻译: 说话人脸(TF)深度伪造生成技术能够从静态源图像和音频信号合成出逼真的面部视频，产生当前基于图像的检测器始终无法识别的伪造内容。与换脸操作不同，TF合成没有可供继承生理特征的真实视频底本，这使得远程光电容积描记法(rPPG)成为该伪造类别独具动机的检测模态。我们提出一个检测框架，通过RhythmFormer提取每段视频的rPPG波形，并训练一组轻量级分类器来区分真实与合成的生理信号。在严格的身份无关协议下对Celeb-DF++的TF子集进行评估（测试身份与训练身份完全分离），我们的1D ResNet取得了0.806的AUC和27.8%的等错误率(EER)，在仅依赖生理通道的情况下，性能与最佳公开发布通用检测器（Effort, ICML 2025）相差仅2.4个百分点。我们记录了对代表性先验rPPG检测器DeepFakesON-Phy的受控复现研究，结果表明其在传统换脸数据上的AUC从0.999下降至Celeb-DF++ TF子集上的0.622。我们进一步证明检测难度强烈依赖于生成方法：七个TF生成器的AUC范围从0.985（Real3DPortrait）到0.690（IP-LAP），且该排名在所有评估协议下完全稳定。这一分布反映了各生成器可解释的生理特性，而非评估噪声，构成了本文的主要理论贡献。

</details>


### [101] [Bounding the Causal Impact of ML-assisted Decision-Making via Counterfactual Correctness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21806)
*Jonathan Zhang,Erik Skalnes,Jacob Chen,Michael Oberst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种部分识别方法，利用先验RCT数据对更新后的机器学习模型的因果效应进行界限估计，通过两种单调性假设（个体级反事实正确性和亚组预测性能与结果的关系）来缩小因果效应的不确定性范围。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 机器学习模型在高风险领域（如医疗、刑事司法）中被广泛用于辅助决策，但其部署后的因果影响评估面临挑战：RCT虽能提供高质量证据，但当模型更新或重新训练以提高预测性能时，重复进行多次RCT通常不可行。因此需要一种方法，能够利用已有的RCT数据来评估新模型的因果效应。

Method: 提出一种部分识别（partial-identification）方法，利用先前的RCT数据对新模型的因果效应构建界限。核心创新在于利用预测精度与下游结果之间的关联假设，通过两种单调性假设：(1) 个体层面的"反事实正确性"——在其它条件相同的情况下，正确的预测会导致非劣的结果；(2) 亚组预测性能与结果之间的关系，可解释为对模型输出信任的假设。

Result: 通过模拟研究验证了所提方法，表明与先前工作相比，纳入这些信息可以产生信息量更丰富的界限（即更紧的因果效应区间）。

Conclusion: 所提出的部分识别方法能够有效地利用现有RCT数据对新模型的因果效应进行界限估计，为解决模型频繁更新场景下的因果评估难题提供了一种可行的方案。

Original Abstract: Predictive machine learning (ML) models are increasingly used to aid human decision-makers across various high-risk domains such as healthcare and criminal justice. There is a growing recognition of the need to evaluate the causal impact of deploying these systems on downstream outcomes, such as patient survival or crime recidivism. Randomized control trials (RCTs) can provide high-quality evidence on the impact of a deployed model, but they run into a challenge: it is often infeasible to run repeated trials when models are updated or retrained to improve predictive performance. In this work, we present a partial-identification approach to using prior RCT data to construct bounds on the causal effect of a new model. The core innovation in our approach is to leverage assumptions relating fine-grained predictive accuracy to downstream outcomes. We do so via two monotonicity assumptions: first, on individual-level `counterfactual correctness' (all else being equal, a correct prediction leads to non-inferior outcomes); and second, on the relation between subgroup predictive performance and outcomes, interpretable as an assumption regarding trust in model outputs. We demonstrate our method with a simulation study, illustrating how incorporating this information can lead to more informative bounds compared to prior work.

中文摘要翻译: 预测性机器学习模型越来越多地被用于辅助人类决策者，涉及医疗、刑事司法等多个高风险领域。人们日益认识到需要评估部署这些系统对下游结果的因果影响，例如患者生存率或犯罪再犯率。随机对照试验（RCT）可以为部署模型的因果效应提供高质量证据，但面临一个挑战：当模型为提高预测性能而被更新或重新训练时，重复进行多次试验通常是不可行的。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种部分识别方法，利用先前的RCT数据对新模型的因果效应构建界限。我们方法的核心创新在于利用关于细粒度预测精度与下游结果之间关系的假设。我们通过两种单调性假设来实现这一点：第一，个体层面的"反事实正确性"（在其它条件相同的情况下，正确的预测导致非劣的结果）；第二，亚组预测性能与结果之间的关系，该假设可解释为对模型输出的信任程度。我们通过模拟研究展示了该方法，说明与先前工作相比，纳入这些信息可以产生信息量更丰富的界限。

</details>


### [102] [Data eccentricity, asymptotics of Gaussian RBF reproducing kernel Hilbert space, and kernel PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21823)
*Sergio A. Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在高斯RBF核的大带宽极限下，其RKHS渐近等距于欧几里得空间；核PCA的特征值、特征投影和主成分均收敛到经典线性PCA，并通过几何偏心度ρ准确预测收敛行为。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究高斯RBF核在大带宽极限下与线性核的渐近等价性，理解核方法（如CKA、核PCA）在该极限下的行为，并找到能预测数据集特定收敛行为的简单可靠指标。

Method: 理论分析：证明高斯RBF核RKHS在大带宽极限下渐近等距于欧几里得空间；分析核PCA的特征值、特征投影和主成分的收敛性；推导出两种核的特征嵌入和PCA正交特征帧相差一个几何相似变换，残差为O((ρ/σ)^2)，其中ρ为数据集几何偏心度（最大与中位成对距离之比）。实验验证：在多类数据集上验证ρ作为收敛行为预测指标的有效性。

Result: 高斯RBF核RKHS在大带宽极限下渐近等距于欧几里得空间；高斯核PCA的特征值、特征投影和主成分均收敛到线性PCA；两种核的RKHS特征嵌入和PCA正交特征帧相差一个几何相似变换，残差阶为O((ρ/σ)^2)；经验证，几何偏心度ρ是数据集特定主方向收敛行为的简单可靠预测指标。

Conclusion: 高斯RBF核在带宽足够大时，其核方法的性能与线性核趋同，且数据集几何偏心度ρ可有效预测具体收敛速度，为核选择和大带宽设置提供理论指导。

Original Abstract: We show that, up to isotropic scaling, the Gaussian RBF reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) is asymptotically isometric to Euclidean space in the large bandwidth limit. This strongly suggests that kernel-based constructions reliant on metric properties of the RKHS will yield results for Gaussian RBF kernels that similarly approach those of linear kernels for large bandwidths. The asymptotic behavior of Gaussian CKA can be understood in this light. We further consider kernel PCA, showing that Gaussian RBF eigenvalues, eigenprojections, and principal components all converge to those of classical (linear) PCA as bandwidth $σ\rightarrow \infty$. For a given data representation, both the RKHS feature embeddings and the orthogonal PCA eigenframes of the two kernel types differ asymptotically by a geometric similarity transformation, up to a residual of size $O \left (\fracρσ \right )^2$, where $ρ$ is a measure of geometric eccentricity of the representation, equal to the ratio of maximum to median pairwise distance between data examples. Experiments over a diverse collection of data sets demonstrate that $ρ$ provides a simple and reliable predictor of dataset-specific convergence behavior in the top principal directions.

中文摘要翻译: 我们证明，在 isotropic 缩放下，高斯RBF再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）在大带宽极限下渐近等距于欧几里得空间。这强烈表明，依赖于RKHS度量性质的核构造方法，对于高斯RBF核将产生与线性核在大带宽下相似的结果。高斯CKA的渐近行为可由此理解。我们进一步考虑核PCA，证明随着带宽σ → ∞，高斯RBF的特征值、特征投影和主成分均收敛到经典（线性）PCA的对应量。对于给定的数据表示，两种核类型的RKHS特征嵌入和正交PCA特征帧在渐近意义下相差一个几何相似变换，残差大小为O((ρ/σ)^2)，其中ρ是表示几何偏心度的度量，等于数据样本间最大与中位成对距离之比。在多种数据集上的实验表明，ρ是主方向上前几个方向上数据集特定收敛行为的一个简单且可靠的预测指标。

</details>


### [103] [A Graph-Based Control Interface for Traffic Signals on Heterogeneous Road Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21831)
*Bertil Braun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于共享图神经网络的交通信号控制界面，通过确定性关联矩阵将运动得分转换为可变大小的合法信号相位集合，实现图大小与动作数量独立于网络参数形状。PPO实验在合成网格、信号覆盖变化和五个异质城市图上验证了其可行性与敏感性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统交通信号控制方法中，图大小和路口特定动作数量与学习网络参数形状耦合，限制了模型在不同拓扑结构交通网络间的泛化能力。本文旨在解耦这种依赖关系，实现跨不同几何结构和信号覆盖范围的迁移能力。

Method: 设计了一个共享图神经网络（GNN），为每个交通运动分配分数；每个路口通过确定性关联矩阵将这些分数转换为自身可变大小的合法信号相位集合。采用有向走廊节点提供交通上下文，运动节点表示受控的输入到输出路径。使用类型化均值聚合产生每个运动的一个标量；相位定义和信号定时保持在学习的网络之外。

Result: PPO实验在未见过的合成网格几何结构上策略保留了性能；信号覆盖变化暴露了对分布偏移的敏感性；单个城市策略实例在五个异质城市图上执行，产生了异质性的结果。

Conclusion: 该界面提供了跨不同交通网络拓扑泛化的可行性证据，但尚不能推广到任意道路网络的一般性估计。图大小和动作数量与网络参数解耦的设计是有效的，但对信号覆盖分布偏移敏感。

Original Abstract: We present a traffic-signal control interface in which a shared graph neural network assigns scores to individual traffic movements. Each junction converts these scores into its own variable-sized set of legal signal phases using a deterministic incidence matrix. Directed corridor nodes provide traffic context, while movement nodes represent controlled input-to-output paths through junctions. Typed mean aggregation produces one scalar per movement; phase definitions and signal timing remain outside the learned network. This makes graph size and junction-specific action count independent of the learned parameter shapes. PPO experiments evaluate the interface on unseen synthetic grid geometries, altered signal coverage, and five heterogeneous city graphs. The policies retained performance across unseen geometries within the synthetic grid family, while changes in signal coverage exposed sensitivity to a signal-coverage distribution shift. A single trained city-policy instance executed across all five city graphs, with heterogeneous outcomes. These results provide feasibility evidence rather than a general estimate of transfer to arbitrary road networks.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种交通信号控制接口，其中共享的图神经网络为单个交通运动分配分数。每个路口通过一个确定性关联矩阵将这些分数转换为自己可变大小的合法信号相位集合。有向走廊节点提供交通上下文，而运动节点表示通过路口的受控输入到输出路径。类型化均值聚合为每个运动产生一个标量；相位定义和信号定时保持在学习的网络之外。这使得图大小和路口特定动作数量独立于学习参数形状。PPO实验在未见过的合成网格几何结构、改变信号覆盖范围以及五个异质城市图上评估了该接口。策略在合成网格族内的未见几何结构上保持了性能，而信号覆盖的变化暴露了对信号覆盖分布偏移的敏感性。一个训练好的城市策略实例在所有五个城市图上执行，产生了异质性的结果。这些结果提供了可行性证据，而非对任意道路网络迁移的一般性估计。

</details>


### [104] [Searching the Space of Feed-Forward Neural-Network Weight-Update Rules with Fixed Depth Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21855)
*Charles Brum,Edward Finkelstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究符号回归能否自动发现比手工设计优化器更优的神经网络权重更新规则。在30个任务中，该方法在25个上超越了最佳调参的传统优化器，平均MSE降低44.47%，证明符号回归是一种发现紧凑优化器变体的轻量级方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探索符号回归能否自动发现超越手工设计优化器的显式神经网络权重更新规则，验证其作为轻量级优化器发现机制的可行性。

Method: 将候选更新规则表示为基于常见优化器（梯度、动量、自适应梯度、矩估计）操作数的固定深度符号表达式，通过符号回归在30个基准/神经网络组合上进行搜索，寻找表现最优的更新规则。

Result: 在30个任务组合中，符号回归发现的更新规则在25个上优于超参数调优后的最佳传统优化器，改进案例的聚合MSE降低44.47%。发现规则虽无统一符号形式，但多结合了自适应归一化、动量项、非线性变换和有理表达式。

Conclusion: 符号回归可以作为一种轻量级机制来发现紧凑的优化器变体，同时还需要更大规模的验证来进一步确认其有效性。

Original Abstract: We investigate whether symbolic regression can discover explicit neural network weight-update rules that outperform standard hand-designed optimizers on small symbolic regression benchmarks. Candidate update rules are represented as fixed-depth symbolic expressions over operands derived from common optimizers, including gradient, momentum, adaptive-gradient, and moment-estimate quantities. Across 30 benchmark/neural network combinations, the symbolic regression procedure found an update rule outperforming the best hyperparameter-tuned established optimizer in 25 cases, with an aggregate MSE reduction of 44.47\% over the improved cases. The discovered rules do not all share a single common symbolic form, but many combine adaptive normalization, momentum-like quantities, nonlinear transformations, and rational expressions. These results suggest that symbolic regression can serve as a lightweight mechanism for discovering compact optimizer variants, while also highlighting the need for larger-scale validation.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了符号回归能否发现显式的神经网络权重更新规则，这些规则在小型符号回归基准上优于标准手工设计的优化器。候选更新规则被表示为基于常见优化器（包括梯度、动量、自适应梯度和矩估计量）操作数的固定深度符号表达式。在30个基准/神经网络组合中，符号回归过程在25个情况下发现了优于最佳超参数调优的已建立优化器的更新规则，改进案例的聚合均方误差降低了44.47%。所发现的规则并不都具有单一的通用符号形式，但许多规则结合了自适应归一化、动量类量、非线性变换和有理表达式。这些结果表明，符号回归可以作为发现紧凑优化器变体的轻量级机制，同时也突显了进行更大规模验证的必要性。

</details>


### [105] [LeAct: Learning to Reason from Expert Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21856)
*Ziran Yang,Chengshuai Shi,Raj Ghugare,Benjamin Eysenbach,Karthik Narasimhan,Chi Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LeAct框架，将专家系统（如博弈引擎、定理证明器）产生的行动作为隐变量，从中恢复自然语言推理链（CoT），从而将专家知识蒸馏到学生模型中。该方法在不完全信息博弈和机器人仿真任务中显著优于现有基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 专家系统（如游戏引擎、经典规划器、定理证明器）在各类领域中能产生近乎最优的行动，但它们只输出行动而不附带推理过程（Chain-of-Thought）。若能恢复这些行动背后的自然语言推理链，就能将专家知识蒸馏到学生模型中，使其泛化到未见过的行动。本文旨在解决如何从专家行动中恢复CoT这一隐变量问题。

Method: 提出LeAct（Learning to reason from Actions）框架。将专家行动背后的CoT视为隐变量，通过优化该隐变量来学习：学生模型为每个专家行动采样候选CoT，保留那些能显著提升该行动恢复概率的CoT，从而迭代优化推理能力。

Result: 在小型可枚举博弈中，LeAct达到求解器数值下限；在更大规模任务中，比最强专家迭代基线接近求解器5倍；在Flop Hold'em（约10^9信息集）中，以+60 mbb/g胜出；在机器人仿真任务中，是唯一优于直接模仿的训练方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一个原则性框架，证明了专家系统可以成为基础模型推理训练的全新来源，为从无声专家中蒸馏推理能力提供了可行路径。

Original Abstract: Modern reasoning models depend on reasoning data, today sourced from human annotations or distilled from stronger LLMs. However, a rich and largely untapped source of supervision lies in expert systems (e.g., game engines, classical planners, theorem provers), which routinely produce near-optimal actions across diverse domains. But these experts are silent: they commit to an action without writing down the chain of thought (CoT) behind it. Recovering that CoT as natural-language reasoning would distill expert knowledge into a student that generalizes beyond the demonstrated actions. We treat it as a latent variable and study how to recover it from the action alone. Our approach, LeAct (Learning to reason from Actions), optimizes this latent variable: the student samples candidate CoTs for each expert action, and we retain those that measurably improve its own probability of recovering the action. Across imperfect-information games at multiple scales and a simulated robotics benchmark, LeAct reaches the solver's numerical floor on small enumerable games. At larger scale, it is $5\times$ closer to the solver than the strongest expert-iteration baseline. At Flop Hold'em ($\sim 10^9$ infosets), LeAct wins head-to-head by $+60$ mbb/g, and on the robotics probe it is the only training recipe that improves on direct imitation. We present a principled framework and the result: expert systems become a categorically new source of reasoning teachers for foundation models.

中文摘要翻译: 现代推理模型依赖于推理数据，这些数据目前来自人工标注或从更强的LLM中蒸馏。然而，一个丰富但尚未充分开发的监督来源在于专家系统（例如游戏引擎、经典规划器、定理证明器），它们能在不同领域中常规地产生近乎最优的行动。但这些专家是沉默的：它们执行行动而不写下背后的推理链（Chain-of-Thought）。恢复以自然语言推理形式呈现的CoT，可以将专家知识蒸馏到能够在演示行动之外进行泛化的学生模型中。我们将CoT视为隐变量，并研究如何仅从行动中恢复它。我们的方法LeAct（Learning to reason from Actions）优化了这一隐变量：学生为每个专家行动采样候选CoT，我们保留那些能显著提升其自身恢复该行动概率的候选CoT。在多尺度不完全信息博弈和模拟机器人基准测试中，LeAct在小型可枚举博弈上达到了求解器的数值下限；在更大规模任务中，它比最强的专家迭代基线更接近求解器5倍；在Flop Hold'em（约10^9信息集）中，LeAct以+60 mbb/g胜出；在机器人探针任务中，它是唯一优于直接模仿的训练方案。本文提出了一个原则性框架，其结果证明：专家系统可以成为基础模型推理训练的一个全新来源。

</details>


### [106] [Scaling Laws for Classical Machine Learning on Tabular Data: A Benchmark Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21866)
*Kaihua Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过127名学生的大规模分布式课堂复现实验，在18个表格数据集和6种模型家族上进行了11,536次训练，拟合了1,648条幂律学习曲线。主要发现：(1) 幂律拟合良好，树集成模型表现最优；(2) 同一模型家族内存在近似的共享指数，但并非数据集无关的普适性；(3) 即使固定随机种子，不同实现间仍存在显著方差（CV(b)=0.144），源于协议中未约束的预处理步骤。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 以往经典ML学习曲线研究仅限于小规模实验（单曲线、单团队、少量数据点），缺乏对幂律拟合在更大规模和多样性条件下的系统验证，也缺少对复现性差异的量化分析。

Method: 在课堂环境中组织127名学生，每人按固定协议在3个指定数据集上运行实验。数据集来自18个表格分类/回归任务，使用6种模型（Boosting、随机森林、SVM、线性/逻辑回归、Ridge、Lasso），拟合形如error(N)=aN^(-b)+c的幂律曲线，共获得11,536次训练和1,648条拟合曲线。

Result: (1) 77.7%的单元格R^2>0.8，Boosting在50%数据集上最优，随机森林33%，线性模型分类任务表现差；(2) 5/6模型家族可用单一共享指数近似拟合跨数据集曲线（R^2差距<0.011），但AIC更偏好非约束拟合，曲线塌缩部分成功（32-58%点在±0.5 dex内），Lasso完全失败，Ridge不稳定；(3) 复现实现间指数变异系数均值CV(b)=0.144，来自未约束的预处理差异。

Conclusion: 幂律学习曲线在表格数据上广泛成立，树模型最优；模型家族内存在近似可预测的压缩性但非普适性；复现性受未标准化预处理步骤影响显著。论文发布了聚合曲线、拟合结果和实际数据需求表。

Original Abstract: Prior classical-ML learning-curve work fits power laws to tree, linear, and kernel models on tabular data, but at small scale: typically one curve, one team, a handful of cells. We present a distributed classroom-scale replication: 127 students each ran a fixed protocol on 3 assigned datasets, drawn from 18 tabular classification and regression datasets and 6 model families (Boosting, Random Forest, SVM, Linear/Logistic, Ridge, Lasso), yielding 11,536 training runs and 1,648 fitted power-law curves of the form error(N) = a N^(-b) + c. Three findings. (1) Power laws fit: R^2 > 0.8 on 77.7% of cells, with tree ensembles dominating at full data (Boosting 50% of datasets, RandomForest 33%; linear models underperform on classification). (2) Approximate shared exponents within a model family: for 5 of 6 families, a single family-level exponent predicts each family's cross-dataset curves nearly as well as per-dataset exponents (R^2 gap < 0.011), though AIC favors the unconstrained fit and curve collapse is partial (32-58% of points within +/-0.5 dex). We frame this as approximate predictive compressibility, not dataset-independent universality; Lasso fails outright (negative control) and Ridge is fragile under leave-one-dataset-out. (3) Replicator-implementation variance: with random_state=42 fixed, independent re-implementations of the same protocol still differ by mean CV(b) = 0.144 on the fitted exponent -- not seed variance, but the spread induced by unconstrained parts of the protocol (preprocessing, encoding, missing-value handling). We release the aggregated curves, per-cell fits, and a practical data-requirement table for N* to reach target error 0.15.

中文摘要翻译: 先前的经典机器学习学习曲线研究将幂律拟合于树模型、线性模型和核模型在表格数据上的表现，但仅限于小规模实验：通常是一条曲线、一个团队、少量数据单元。我们进行了一项分布式的课堂规模复现：127名学生各自按照固定协议在3个指定数据集上运行实验，数据集来自18个表格分类和回归数据集以及6个模型家族（Boosting、随机森林、SVM、线性/逻辑回归、Ridge、Lasso），共产生11,536次训练运行和1,648条形式为error(N)=aN^(-b)+c的拟合幂律曲线。三个发现：(1) 幂律拟合良好：77.7%的单元格R^2>0.8，树集成模型在全数据量上表现最优（Boosting在50%数据集上最优，随机森林33%；线性模型在分类任务上表现不佳）。(2) 同一模型家族内存在近似的共享指数：对于6个家族中的5个，单一家族级指数预测每个家族跨数据集曲线的效果几乎与逐数据集指数一样好（R^2差距<0.011），尽管AIC偏向于非约束拟合且曲线塌缩是部分的（32-58%的点在±0.5 dex范围内）。我们将其框架化为近似预测可压缩性，而非数据集无关的普适性；Lasso完全失败（负对照）而Ridge在留一数据集验证下不稳定。(3) 复现者实现的方差：固定random_state=42后，同一协议的独立复现实现之间，拟合指数上的变异系数均值仍达到CV(b)=0.144——这不是随机种子方差，而是由协议中未约束部分（预处理、编码、缺失值处理）引起的分散。我们发布了聚合曲线、逐单元格拟合结果以及达到目标误差0.15所需的N*实用数据需求表。

</details>


### [107] [Variance-Reduced Q-Learning over Static and Time-Varying Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21876)
*Sreejeet Maity,Feng Zhu,Aritra Mitra,Robert W. Heath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体在相同MDP上协同学习的分布式强化学习问题，提出了一种基于epoch的分布式Q-learning算法VRDQ，利用共识协议实现信息扩散，在静态和时变网络上均实现了线性加速收敛，且通信代价仅为$\tilde{O}(1)$，显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有分布式强化学习算法虽然能实现样本效率上的线性加速，但通常需要大量通信开销，这在实际分布式系统中是一个关键瓶颈。本文旨在设计一种既能保持线性加速收敛优势，又能大幅降低通信成本的分布式Q-learning算法。

Method: 提出VRDQ算法，采用基于epoch的分布式Q-learning框架：在每个epoch内，智能体本地估计Bellman最优性算子，并通过基于共识的协议在网络中扩散信息。算法适用于静态网络和时变网络两种场景。

Result: 在静态和时变网络上，VRDQ均实现了具有高概率有限时间收敛率的性能，并且收敛率享受协作带来的线性加速。关键结果是，实现这种样本复杂度上的线性加速仅需要$\tilde{O}(1)$的通信代价，大幅优于先前工作。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在分布式强化学习中，协作带来的样本效率线性加速可以在极低的通信开销下实现，为设计通信高效的分布式强化学习算法提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: We investigate a decentralized reinforcement learning problem involving multiple agents that interact with the same Markov Decision Process (MDP). The agents can exchange information over a network to collectively learn the optimal state-action value function. For this setting, we introduce a novel epoch-based distributed $Q$-learning algorithm called VRDQ, where within each epoch, agents locally estimate the Bellman optimality operator and diffuse information using a consensus-based protocol. For both static and time-varying networks, we establish high-probability finite-time convergence rates for VRDQ that enjoy linear speedups from collaboration. Crucially, we prove that such speedups in sample-complexity require only $\tilde{O}(1)$ communication, substantially improving upon the communication costs in prior work.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了一个去中心化强化学习问题，涉及多个与同一马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）交互的智能体。智能体可以通过网络交换信息，以共同学习最优状态-动作价值函数。针对这一设定，我们引入了一种新颖的基于epoch的分布式Q-learning算法，称为VRDQ，在每个epoch内，智能体本地估计Bellman最优性算子，并使用基于共识的协议扩散信息。对于静态网络和时变网络，我们都建立了VRDQ的高概率有限时间收敛率，该收敛率享有协作带来的线性加速。关键的是，我们证明实现样本复杂度上的这种线性加速仅需要$\tilde{O}(1)$的通信，显著优于先前工作中的通信代价。

</details>


### [108] [MissHyper: Restoring Clinical Synchronicity in Missingness-Guided Hypergraph Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21922)
*Mingyi Ma,Qingxiong Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MissHyper模型，通过在消息传递前恢复共时间戳上下文（快照恢复、支持密度编码、缺失引导门控），解决临床不规则多变量时间序列中事件初始化瓶颈问题，在PhysioNet 2012、MIMIC-III/IV上实现多步预测的持续提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有事件中心模型过早扁平化共时间戳结构，将同一时刻采集的测量嵌入为孤立节点，导致局部患者状态上下文在后续消息传播层之前不可用，存在预传播表示瓶颈。

Method: 提出MissHyper模型：1) 支持密度编码为每个事件增强局部支持密度线索；2) 快照恢复聚合共时间戳记录以重建患者状态上下文；3) 缺失引导门控自适应融合节点级证据与恢复的上下文信息。

Result: 在PhysioNet 2012、MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV三个数据集上，MissHyper在多步预测中取得一致提升，超越强超图基线模型。消融实验表明快照恢复、自适应融合和支持密度编码均有贡献。

Conclusion: 改善事件初始化能在不重新设计下游传播架构的情况下提升稀疏临床预测性能，事件初始化是稀疏临床预测的关键设计维度。

Original Abstract: Clinical irregular multivariate time series are shaped not only by physiological dynamics but also by the measurement process that determines when and what to observe. In event-centric models, however, co-timestamp structure can be flattened too early: measurements acquired at the same timestamp are embedded as isolated nodes, leaving local patient-state context unavailable until later message-passing layers. We study this pre-propagation representation bottleneck and address it by restoring co-timestamp context before message passing begins. We propose MissHyper, a missingness-guided hypergraph forecasting model with pre-propagation synchronicity restoration. MissHyper augments each event with a local support-density cue, aggregates co-timestamp records to recover patient-state context, and uses a missingness-guided gate to adaptively fuse node-specific evidence with the recovered context. Across PhysioNet 2012, MIMIC-III, and MIMIC-IV, MissHyper achieves consistent gains in multi-step forecasting and outperforms a strong hypergraph baseline. These results suggest that improving event initialization can benefit sparse clinical forecasting without requiring a redesigned downstream propagation architecture. Ablations indicate that snapshot restoration, adaptive fusion, and support-density encoding all contribute, pointing to event initialization as a critical design axis for sparse clinical forecasting.

中文摘要翻译: 临床不规则多变量时间序列不仅受生理动力学影响，还受决定何时观察什么的测量过程影响。然而，在事件中心模型中，共时间戳结构可能过早被扁平化：同一时间戳获取的测量被嵌入为孤立节点，导致局部患者状态上下文在后续消息传播层之前不可用。我们研究了这种预传播表示瓶颈，并通过在消息传递开始之前恢复共时间戳上下文来解决它。我们提出MissHyper，一种缺失引导的超图预测模型，具有预传播同步恢复功能。MissHyper为每个事件增强局部支持密度线索，聚合共时间戳记录以恢复患者状态上下文，并使用缺失引导门控自适应融合节点特定证据与恢复的上下文。在PhysioNet 2012、MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上，MissHyper在多步预测中取得一致提升，并超越强超图基线。这些结果表明，改进事件初始化可以在不需要重新设计下游传播架构的情况下有益于稀疏临床预测。消融实验表明，快照恢复、自适应融合和支持密度编码均有贡献，指出事件初始化是稀疏临床预测的关键设计维度。

</details>


### [109] [RIS-Kernel: A Model-Agnostic Architecture for Long-Context LLM Inference via Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21927)
*Anderson R. Santos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种模型无关的稀疏注意力推理引擎RIS，通过随机稀疏几何将自注意力复杂度从O(N²)降至O(N log N)，在商品级CPU上实现长上下文LLM推理，在65K token下超越零上下文基线14个百分点。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 全自注意力的O(N²)复杂度限制了LLM的长上下文文档分析能力（通常不超过65,536 token），且需要昂贵的GPU集群。研究旨在降低注意力计算复杂度，使长上下文推理在普通硬件上可行。

Method: 提出Reduced Interaction Sampling (RIS)推理引擎，是一种模型无关的架构，无需修改权重。通过稀疏随机几何将自注意力复杂度从O(N²)降低到O(N log N)，使其可运行在商品级内存限制内。包括两种变体：RIS-Stochastic（随机采样）和RIS-Structural（结构化稀疏）。

Result: 在Qwen2-1.5B-Instruct上验证：(1) 32K token下，1%密度+70个种子的RIS-Stochastic达到75.00%准确率，超越原生密集基线(71.88%)；5%密度+10个种子与之持平(71.88%)。(2) 稀疏注意力充当正则化器，低密度多种子滤除噪声，高密度重新引入干扰噪声。(3) 65K token下密集注意力OOM，RIS相比零上下文基线(51.56%)提升14.06个百分点（McNemar检验p=0.078）。全部在无GPU的普通CPU服务器上运行。

Conclusion: 长上下文LLM推理可以在无GPU加速的标准学术硬件上可行，RIS通过稀疏随机几何实现了有效的注意力正则化，在降低计算成本的同时甚至能超越密集基线性能。

Original Abstract: Full self-attention in large language models scales as O(N^2), which limits long-context document analysis to 65,536 tokens and requires costly GPU clusters. The Reduced Interaction Sampling (RIS) inference engine addresses this constraint as a model-agnostic architecture. Without modifying weights, RIS reduces self-attention complexity to O(N log N) using sparse stochastic geometry that fits within commodity memory limits. We validate RIS on Qwen2-1.5B-Instruct across two regimes. In controlled evaluations at 32,768 tokens (where native dense attention serves as the upper bound), RIS-Stochastic at 1% density and 70 ensemble seeds achieves 75.00% accuracy, outperforming the native dense baseline (71.88%), while RIS-Stochastic at 5% density and 10 seeds matches it (71.88%). This demonstrates that sparse attention acts as a regularizer: low density (1%) over multiple seeds filters out sequence-level noise, whereas higher density (5%) reintroduces distractor noise. Under the tightest budget, RIS-Structural reaches 68.75% accuracy at 1% density with just 10 seeds, recovering 75% of the contextual gap relative to the zero-context floor (59.38%). At 65,536 tokens, where dense attention triggers out-of-memory faults, RIS yields retrieval gains of up to 14.06 percentage points over the zero-context floor (51.56%), which is confirmed as marginally significant under McNemar's paired test (p = 0.078 < 0.10). All evaluations run on commodity, unaccelerated CPU servers (16-128 GB of RAM), demonstrating that long-context LLM inference is feasible on standard academic hardware without GPU acceleration.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型中的全自注意力机制的计算复杂度为O(N²)，这将其长上下文文档分析能力限制在65,536个token以内，并且需要昂贵的GPU集群。本文提出的减少交互采样（RIS）推理引擎作为一种模型无关的架构解决了这一限制。在不修改权重的情况下，RIS利用稀疏随机几何将自注意力复杂度降至O(N log N)，使其能够适配商品级内存限制。我们在Qwen2-1.5B-Instruct上对RIS进行了两个阶段的验证。在受控评估中（32,768个token，此时原生密集注意力作为上界），密度为1%、集成70个种子的RIS-Stochastic达到了75.00%的准确率，超过了原生密集基线（71.88%）；而密度为5%、10个种子的RIS-Stochastic与之持平（71.88%）。这表明稀疏注意力起到了正则化器的作用：低密度（1%）多种子设置滤除了序列级噪声，而较高密度（5%）则重新引入了干扰噪声。在最严格的预算下，密度为1%、仅10个种子的RIS-Structural达到了68.75%的准确率，恢复了相对于零上下文基线（59.38%）75%的上下文差距。在65,536个token时，密集注意力触发内存溢出故障，RIS相对于零上下文基线（51.56%）获得了高达14.06个百分点的检索增益，McNemar配对检验确认其具有边缘显著性（p = 0.078 < 0.10）。所有评估均在商品级、无加速的CPU服务器（16-128 GB RAM）上运行，证明了长上下文LLM推理可以在无GPU加速的标准学术硬件上实现。

</details>


### [110] [LatentFlow: Visual Analytics for Latent Space Analysis in Molecular Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21941)
*Shiyi Liu,Jiaqing Chen,Nicholas Hadler,Rostyslav Hnatyshyn,Michael W. Mahoney,Talita Perciano,John F. Hartwig,Gunther H. Weber,Ross Maciejewski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LatentFlow是一个可视化分析系统，通过改进的Sankey图跟踪分子图神经网络中嵌入向量的聚类变化，帮助科学家分析潜在空间在层间和模型状态间的演化，从而理解模型行为并识别有意义的化学模式。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法在分析分子GNN潜在空间时，缺乏跨层、跨模型状态（如训练轮次、模型配置和输入数据）的支持，难以理解潜在空间如何随模型演化以及与化学概念的关联。

Method: LatentFlow将嵌入向量聚类，使用改进的Sankey图追踪聚类在层间和模型状态间的变化，并将聚类关联到代表性分子及其共享子结构，同时允许科学家引入领域知识与潜在空间模式进行对比。

Result: 通过两个案例研究的评估，LatentFlow能够帮助科学家理解潜在空间的演化过程，识别有意义的分子模式，并更好地解释模型行为。

Conclusion: LatentFlow有效支持了分子GNN潜在空间的多维度分析，为科学家提供了理解模型内部化学信息组织方式的可视化分析工具。

Original Abstract: Chemists and materials scientists increasingly use machine learning models, such as graph neural networks (GNNs), to predict properties of molecules and the outcomes of their reactions. Beyond predictive performance, understanding how these models organize chemical information internally in their latent spaces, i.e., the embeddings of the molecules, is critical. Analyzing latent spaces helps diagnose model behavior and assess whether the learned embeddings are organized in ways that reflect meaningful chemical relationships. Unfortunately, existing methods provide limited support for analyzing latent spaces across layers and across different model states (e.g., training epochs, model configurations, and input data), making it difficult to understand how these latent spaces evolve throughout a model or relate to chemical concepts. We present LatentFlow, a visual analytics system developed in collaboration with a domain expert for analyzing latent spaces in molecular GNNs. LatentFlow groups embeddings into clusters and supports exploration of latent spaces by tracking how these clusters change across layers and model states using a modified Sankey diagram. To support interpretation, LatentFlow links these clusters to representative molecules and their shared substructures, and it allows scientists to introduce their own domain knowledge and compare it with the patterns found in the latent spaces. We evaluate LatentFlow through two case studies. The results show that LatentFlow helps scientists understand how latent spaces evolve, identify meaningful molecular patterns, and better interpret model behavior.

中文摘要翻译: 化学家和材料科学家越来越多地使用机器学习模型（如图神经网络）来预测分子性质及其反应结果。除了预测性能之外，理解这些模型如何在潜在空间（即分子的嵌入向量）中内部组织化学信息至关重要。分析潜在空间有助于诊断模型行为，并评估学习到的嵌入是否以反映有意义的化学关系的方式进行组织。不幸的是，现有方法在支持跨层和跨不同模型状态（如训练轮次、模型配置和输入数据）分析潜在空间方面能力有限，这使得理解这些潜在空间如何在模型中演化以及与化学概念的关联变得困难。我们提出LatentFlow，一个与领域专家合作开发的可视化分析系统，用于分析分子GNN中的潜在空间。LatentFlow将嵌入向量聚集成簇，并使用改进的Sankey图追踪这些簇在层间和模型状态间的变化来支持潜在空间的探索。为了支持解释，LatentFlow将这些簇与代表性分子及其共享子结构关联起来，并允许科学家引入自己的领域知识，与潜在空间中发现的特征进行对比。我们通过两个案例研究评估了LatentFlow。结果表明，LatentFlow帮助科学家理解潜在空间如何演化，识别有意义的分子模式，并更好地解释模型行为。

</details>


### [111] [Multi-Agent Debate and Visual Information Extraction for SeePhys Pro: A 1st-Place Technical Report from ICML 2026 AI4Math Track 3 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21946)
*Jiseok Kwak,Suhyeon Jo,Taewoo Kim,Yeongmin Kim,Byeonghu Na,Il-chul Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个两阶段框架解决大学物理视觉问题：先通过视觉信息提取将图像内容转化为文本，再通过多智能体辩论协调三个求解器进行推理。在SeePhys Pro赛道获得第一名，准确率从0.643提升至0.802。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当问题的关键信息包含在图像而非文本中时，大语言模型在视觉物理问题上的表现大幅下降，且这种模态差距随问题更多信息迁移到图像中而扩大。现有方法难以弥合这一模态鸿沟。

Method: 两阶段框架：1）视觉信息提取阶段：将图像中的图表内容重新表达为求解器可读的文本，以缩小模态差距；2）推理阶段：通过多智能体辩论协调三个异构求解器进行答案选择。

Result: 在公开数据集上，整体准确率从单智能体基线的0.643提升至0.802；在私有数据集上整体准确率达0.743，获得公开榜和私有榜双料第一名。分析发现：性能提升主要来自可靠的答案选择而非额外的辩论；图像辅助的价值与问题信息被锁定在图像中的程度成正比。

Conclusion: 通过视觉信息提取缩小模态差距，再通过多智能体协调进行可靠答案选择，能有效提升大语言模型解决大学物理视觉问题的能力。

Original Abstract: This technical report presents our approach to Challenge Track~3: SeePhys Pro at the 3rd AI for Math Workshop, where the task is to answer college-level physics questions whose statement and figure may be given partly or entirely as an image. Visual physics problems become substantially harder for large language models when the decisive information resides in a figure rather than in the text, and this modality gap widens as more of the problem migrates into the image. We address the task with a two-stage framework: a visual information extraction stage that re-expresses figure content as solver-readable text to close the modality gap, and a reasoning stage that orchestrates three heterogeneous solvers through multi-agent debate. Our analysis yields two findings: the gain from orchestration comes from reliable answer selection rather than from additional debate, and the value of a figure aid scales with how much of the problem is locked inside the image. The resulting pipeline improves overall accuracy over a single-agent baseline from 0.643 to 0.802 on the public split, and won 1st place on both the public and the private leaderboard (private overall 0.743).

中文摘要翻译: 本技术报告介绍了我们在第三届AI for数学工作坊挑战赛道3：SeePhys Pro中的方法。该赛道的任务是回答大学水平的物理问题，其中问题和图像的陈述可能部分或全部以图像形式给出。当关键信息存在于图像而非文本中时，视觉物理问题对大型语言模型来说变得相当困难，并且随着更多问题信息迁移到图像中，这种模态差距进一步扩大。我们采用两阶段框架来解决该任务：视觉信息提取阶段，将图像内容重新表达为求解器可读的文本以缩小模态差距；推理阶段，通过多智能体辩论协调三个异构求解器。我们的分析得出两个发现：协调带来的性能提升来自可靠的答案选择而非额外的辩论；图像辅助的价值与问题被锁定在图像中的程度成正比。最终的流水线将公开数据集上的整体准确率从单智能体基线的0.643提升至0.802，并在公开榜和私有榜上均获得第一名（私有数据集整体准确率为0.743）。

</details>


### [112] [MA-DAR: Manifold-Aligned Dynamic Adaptive Routing for Continual Temporal Knowledge Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21949)
*Xiangjun Shi,Chong Mu,Jinchuan Zhang,Lizong Zhang,Yuefeng He,Shang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MA-DAR框架，通过流形对齐、动态自适应路由和极化正则化，解决持续时序知识图谱推理中回放表示与当前表示的表示冲突（范数主导和语义模糊）问题，显著提升推理性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于回放的持续时序知识图谱推理方法主要关注回放什么，而忽视了回放表示如何与当前表示融合，直接融合会导致表示冲突（范数主导和语义模糊），从而降低持续推理性能。

Method: 1) 流形对齐：将回放表示和当前表示对齐到共享流形，缓解分布差异；2) 动态门控机制：学习维度级融合权重，自适应确定两者对融合表示的贡献；3) 极化正则化：抑制模糊门控决策，促进更果断的路由行为，实现更稳定的知识融合。

Result: 在四个公共持续TKG基准数据集上，MA-DAR一致提升了代表性TKG编码器的性能，在不同回放设置下均保持有效；消融实验和可视化分析验证了流形对齐和动态自适应路由在缓解表示冲突和改进持续推理方面的有效性。

Conclusion: MA-DAR作为一种轻量级即插即用框架，通过流形对齐和动态自适应路由有效缓解持续时序知识图谱推理中的表示冲突问题，显著提升持续推理性能，且具有良好的兼容性和泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Continual temporal knowledge graph (TKG) reasoning aims to continuously incorporate newly emerging facts while preserving previously acquired knowledge. Replay-based continual learning has achieved promising performance by revisiting historical representations. However, existing methods primarily focus on what to replay, while largely overlooking how replayed representations should be integrated with current ones. Such direct integration often gives rise to two critical forms of representation conflict: \textit{norm domination} and \textit{semantic blurring}, ultimately degrading continual reasoning performance. To address these challenges, we propose MA-DAR (Manifold-Aligned Dynamic Adaptive Routing), a lightweight plug-and-play framework for replay representation fusion. MA-DAR first aligns replayed and current representations onto a shared manifold to alleviate distribution discrepancies. It then employs a dynamic gating mechanism to learn dimension-wise fusion weights, adaptively determining the contribution of replayed and current representations to the fused representation. Furthermore, a polarization regularizer encourages more decisive routing behaviors by discouraging ambiguous gating decisions, resulting in more stable and effective knowledge integration. Extensive experiments on four public continual TKG benchmarks demonstrate that MA-DAR consistently improves the performance of representative TKG encoders while remaining effective under different replay settings. Comprehensive ablation studies and visualization analyses further verify the effectiveness of manifold alignment and dynamic adaptive routing in mitigating representation conflicts and improving continual reasoning.

中文摘要翻译: 持续时序知识图谱推理旨在持续融入新出现的事实，同时保留先前获得的知识。基于回放的持续学习方法通过重访历史表示取得了令人瞩目的性能。然而，现有方法主要关注回放什么，而很大程度上忽视了回放表示应如何与当前表示进行融合。这种直接融合常常引发两种关键的表示冲突：范数主导和语义模糊，最终导致持续推理性能下降。为解决这些挑战，我们提出了MA-DAR（流形对齐的动态自适应路由），一种用于回放表示融合的轻量级即插即用框架。MA-DAR首先将回放表示和当前表示对齐到共享流形上，以缓解分布差异；然后采用动态门控机制学习维度级的融合权重，自适应地确定回放表示和当前表示对融合表示的贡献；此外，极化正则化器通过抑制模糊的门控决策来鼓励更果断的路由行为，从而实现更稳定有效的知识融合。在四个公共持续TKG基准上的大量实验表明，MA-DAR持续提升了代表性TKG编码器的性能，并在不同回放设置下保持有效。全面的消融研究和可视化分析进一步验证了流形对齐和动态自适应路由在缓解表示冲突和改进持续推理方面的有效性。

</details>


### [113] [Teaching LLMs to Self-Evolve: Cultivating Core Meta-Skills with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21971)
*Shujin Wu,Cheng Qian,Xiusi Chen,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MetaEvolve通过训练元技能（如自我反思和环境反馈利用）来增强AI模型的自我进化能力，在代码生成任务上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有后训练方法忽视了元技能（如自我反思与环境反馈）对多轮迭代优化的关键作用，限制了模型在测试时通过自我进化持续提升性能的能力。

Method: 提出MetaEvolve框架，核心包括：(1) 数据合成流水线，生成包含当前程序、适应度分数（正确性与效率）和历史尝试的演化轨迹；(2) 基于程序执行的连续奖励信号，使用进化感知的强化学习进行训练；(3) 推理阶段的进化搜索。

Result: 在7个代码基准上，MetaEvolve在分布内任务上比最强基线高出10.01%，在分布外任务上高出24.12%；在训练域外的开放算法优化问题上实现46.9%的相对提升。

Conclusion: 明确培养自我进化的元技能是实现更强大、能自主自我进化的AI的一条有原则的路径。

Original Abstract: Test-time scaling through iterative self-evolution with environment feedback, as demonstrated by AlphaEvolve, shows remarkable performance gains. We hypothesize that the success of such evolution frameworks hinges on meta-skills, such as self-reflection with environment feedback, that enable effective multi-round refinement, yet are largely neglected by traditional post-training. To bridge this gap, we present MetaEvolve, a framework designed to develop these meta-skills via a data synthesis pipeline, evolution-aware reinforcement learning (RL), and inference-time evolutionary search. Concretely, we ground MetaEvolve in coding, where program execution provides natural, continuous reward signals beyond binary correctness. Building on these signals, we synthesize evolution trajectories as training data, each containing a current program, its fitness score (combining correctness and efficiency), and a history of prior attempts, and train the model via RL with verifiable rewards derived from test case execution. By training on large-scale code data, we aim to inspire generalizable domain-agnostic meta-skills that can transfer broadly to open-ended problems where such rich training signals are scarce. Across seven coding benchmarks, MetaEvolve outperforms the strongest baseline by 10.01% absolute on in-distribution tasks and 24.12% on out-of-distribution tasks. On open-ended algorithm optimization problems entirely outside the training domain, it further achieves a 46.9% relative improvement.
