We identify two policy-dependent conditions: spectral separation of the predictable signal governs representation identifiability, while non-degenerate conditional action variation governs transition identifiability. We prove that when both conditions hold, every global minimizer of the JEPA objective identifies the latent state and controlled transition up to an orthogonal transformation. We further derive quantitative bounds on representation and transition identifiability under approximate optimization. Finally, we construct predictor perturbations along weakly excited action directions whose counterfactual-to-on-policy error ratio is the inverse transition-identifiability margin, revealing the cost of limited action coverage. Experiments across nonlinear observation maps and behavior policies corroborate the theory and demonstrate implications for transition identifiability, counterfactual prediction, and goal-conditioned latent planning.

中文摘要翻译: 学习能够从高维观测中推断环境动态并在候选动作下预测结果的世界模型，是规划与控制的核心。联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）提供了在表征空间中学习此类模型的引人注目的框架。最近的条件动作扩展在视觉控制和潜在空间规划中表现良好，但留下了一个根本问题未解决：受控潜在预测何时能够同时识别底层状态和受控动力学？在非线性观测和条件动作变化有限的行为策略下，这一问题具有挑战性，因为状态依赖的演化与动作效应在统计上可能混杂。我们针对具有高斯潜在状态和高斯行为策略的受控世界模型建立了联合可辨识性理论。我们确定了两种依赖策略的条件：可预测信号的谱分离程度控制表征的可辨识性，而非退化的条件动作变化控制转移的可辨识性。我们证明，当两个条件均满足时，JEPA目标的每个全局最优解都能正交等价地识别潜在状态和受控转移。我们进一步推导了近似优化下表征和转移可辨识性的定量界。最后，我们沿弱激励动作方向构造了预测器扰动，其反事实与在线策略误差比为转移可辨识性边际的倒数，揭示了有限动作覆盖的代价。在非线性观测映射和行为策略上的实验验证了该理论，并展示了其对转移可辨识性、反事实预测和目标条件潜在规划的启示。

</details>


### [143] [Hyperball May Not Be a Free Lunch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22444)
*Yihao Xiao,Jialong Sun,Zitian Gao,Zeming Wei,Chutian Wang,Ran Tao,Jiaye Teng,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Hyperball风格优化器在大规模训练中的优势来源进行深入分析。通过推导角度有效学习率、分解优化器更新的径向和切向分量，以及设计启发式实验，发现Hyperball风格优化器（如MuonH与MuonWD）的性能差异主要源于有效步长演化，而非其内在的更新方向优势。同时指出保持恒定角速度并不能消除学习率调度问题，谨慎调度对发挥其潜力至关重要。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Hyperball风格优化器通过固定矩阵参数范数并归一化更新，在大规模训练中表现出优越性能，但其优势来源尚不明确。本文旨在从理论角度揭示其工作机制。

Method: 1. 从参数状态间的角位移出发，推导包含参数-更新角度、参数范数和更新范数的角度有效学习率。2. 将优化器更新分解为径向分量和切向分量，分析径向更新对一步角位移的影响。3. 设计启发式实验，仅修改学习率调度使不同优化器的动力学相互复现。

Result: 1. 径向分量对角度有效学习率的直接影响有限，无法解释MuonH与MuonWD的训练速度差异。2. 两者差异主要源于有效步长的演化，而非Hyperball带来的更新方向优势。3. 更强的学习率衰减可加速早期训练，但可能损害后期性能。

Conclusion: 保持恒定角速度并不能消除学习率调度问题，谨慎的学习率调度对发挥Hyperball风格优化器的潜力至关重要。

Original Abstract: For scale-invariant deep networks, Hyperball-style optimizers have shown strong performance in large-scale training by fixing the norms of matrix-valued parameters and normalizing updates. However, the source of their advantage remains unclear. Starting from the angular displacement between consecutive parameter states, we derive an angular effective learning rate that accounts for the parameter-update angle, parameter norm, and update norm. We also show that the conventional norm-based measure is a special case under parameter-update orthogonality. We then decompose optimizer updates into radial and tangential components and analyze how radial updates affect one-step angular displacement. Under the training configurations considered, numerical results show that the radial component has only a limited direct effect on the angular effective learning rate. It therefore cannot explain why MuonH converges more slowly than MuonWD early in training but overtakes it later. To further isolate the underlying mechanism, we devise a heuristic experiment that modifies only the learning-rate schedule so that the dynamics of each optimizer reproduce those of the other. The results suggest that their main difference stems from the evolution of the effective step size rather than an intrinsically superior update direction induced by Hyperball. Our pretraining experiments further show that more aggressive learning-rate decay can accelerate MuonH early in training but may impair its later performance. Thus, maintaining a constant angular velocity does not eliminate the learning-rate-scheduling problem; careful scheduling remains essential to realizing the potential of Hyperball-style optimizers. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/mangocrazz/hyperball-may-not-be-a-free-lunch.

中文摘要翻译: 对于尺度不变的深度网络，Hyperball风格优化器通过固定矩阵参数范数并归一化更新，在大规模训练中展现出强大性能。然而，其优势来源尚不明确。从连续参数状态之间的角位移出发，我们推导了一个考虑参数-更新角度、参数范数和更新范数的角度有效学习率。我们还证明了传统的基于范数的度量是参数-更新正交条件下的特例。随后，我们将优化器更新分解为径向分量和切向分量，并分析径向更新如何影响一步角位移。在所考虑的训练配置下，数值结果表明径向分量对角度有效学习率的直接影响有限。因此，它无法解释为何MuonH在训练早期收敛慢于MuonWD，但后期反超。为进一步隔离内在机制，我们设计了一个启发式实验，仅修改学习率调度，使每个优化器的动力学复现另一个优化器的动力学。结果表明，二者的主要差异源于有效步长的演化，而非Hyperball带来的内在优越更新方向。我们的预训练实验进一步表明，更激进的学习率衰减可以在训练早期加速MuonH，但可能损害其后期性能。因此，保持恒定角速度并不能消除学习率调度问题；谨慎的调度对于发挥Hyperball风格优化器的潜力仍然至关重要。我们的代码公开在https://github.com/mangocrazz/hyperball-may-not-be-a-free-lunch。

</details>


### [144] [Phylogenetic signal in marine mammal and bird vocalizations captured by audio foundation models: the limited benefit of domain-specific pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22458)
*Víctor Rincón Yepes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究预训练音频模型（AST、CLAP、BEATs-bio、BirdNET）在未显式训练的情况下，其嵌入表示是否隐含了物种间的系统发育（进化）距离信息。通过海洋哺乳动物（32种，1754条录音）和鸟类（20种）两个独立辐射类群的曼特尔检验，发现基础模型能够显著恢复系统发育信号（如海洋哺乳动物中CLAP和BEATs-bio的r=0.82），远超手工MFCC特征，且领域专用预训练并非必要条件。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 验证预训练音频模型是否在无监督条件下自发学习到了超越显式标注标签的深层结构信息——具体而言，即音频嵌入空间的几何结构是否隐含了物种间的系统发育关系。

Method: 采用下游探测任务范式：使用四种预训练音频模型（AST、CLAP、BEATs-bio、BirdNET）提取物种发声的嵌入特征，通过曼特尔检验（Mantel test）和偏曼特尔检验（partial Mantel test）评估嵌入空间中的声学距离与系统发育距离之间的相关性。在海洋哺乳动物（32种，Watkins数据库）和鸟类（20种，Jetz系统发育树）两个独立进化辐射类群上验证，并控制嵌入维度（PCA投影至105维）和主频等混淆因素。

Result: (1) 海洋哺乳动物中，CLAP和BEATs-bio的r=0.82，AST的r=0.74（均p<0.001），而手工MFCC特征无显著相关（r=0.040, p=0.338）；(2) PCA降维至105维后差距依然存在，排除表示尺寸假象；(3) 偏曼特尔检验控制主频后仍保持97%解释方差（r=0.404），排除音高伪装；(4) 鸟类中AST（r=0.55）和CLAP（r=0.52）表现优于领域专用模型BirdNET和BEATs-bio（r≈0.32-0.36）。

Conclusion: 预训练音频嵌入在两个独立进化辐射类群中均携带了显著的系统发育信息，且领域专用预训练并非这种进化信号涌现的必要条件。通用基础模型在恢复系统发育关系方面甚至优于领域专用模型。

Original Abstract: Do learned audio embeddings encode structure that nobody told them to encode? We probe four large pretrained audio models (AST, CLAP, BEATs-bio and BirdNET) with a downstream task none of them saw during training: recovering phylogenetic distance from species vocalizations. If the geometry of the embedding space tracks the tree of life, the representation is picking up something deeper than the labels the model was optimized for.
  We run Mantel tests across two independent radiations. In 32 marine mammal species (1,754 recordings from the Watkins Marine Mammal Sound Database) the foundation models recover strong phylogenetic signal within the 26 cetaceans (CLAP r=0.82, BEATs-bio r=0.82, AST r=0.74; all p<0.001), among the highest acoustic-phylogenetic correlations reported for any taxon. Hand-crafted MFCC features (105d) find nothing (r=0.040, p=0.338). The gap survives after PCA-projecting every embedding down to 105 dimensions, so it is not an artefact of representation size. It also survives a partial Mantel test controlling for dominant frequency (partial Mantel r=0.404, keeping 97% of the variance explained), so it is not just pitch in disguise.
  We repeat the analysis on 20 bird species using the Jetz et al. (2012) phylogeny, and this time add BirdNET, a classifier trained end-to-end on around 6,000 bird species. The general-purpose foundation models recover the signal again (AST r=0.55, CLAP r=0.52). The unexpected result is that neither BirdNET nor the bioacoustic BEATs-bio beat them (r around 0.32 to 0.36). Matching the training domain to the target taxon does not, by itself, help. Pretrained audio embeddings carry evolutionary information across two independent radiations, and domain-specific pretraining is not required for it to emerge.

中文摘要翻译: 学习到的音频嵌入是否会编码没有人明确告诉它们要编码的结构？我们使用四个大型预训练音频模型（AST、CLAP、BEATs-bio和BirdNET）对一项训练期间均未见过的下游任务进行探测：从物种发声信号中恢复系统发育距离。如果嵌入空间的几何结构能够追踪生命之树，那么表征所捕捉到的信息就比模型优化所依据的标签更为深层。我们在两个独立的辐射类群上进行了曼特尔检验。在32个海洋哺乳动物物种（1,754条录音，来自Watkins海洋哺乳动物声音数据库）中，基础模型在26个鲸类物种中恢复了强烈的系统发育信号（CLAP r=0.82，BEATs-bio r=0.82，AST r=0.74；均p<0.001），这是迄今报道的任何分类群中声学-系统发育相关性的最高水平之一。手工设计的MFCC特征（105维）未发现任何信号（r=0.040, p=0.338）。将每个嵌入主成分分析投影到105维后，该差距仍然存在，因此并非表征尺寸的假象。在控制主频的偏曼特尔检验中（偏曼特尔r=0.404，保持97%的解释方差），该差距也仍然存在，因此也不仅仅是音高在伪装。我们使用Jetz等人（2012）的系统发育树在20个鸟类物种上重复了该分析，并增加了BirdNET——一个在约6,000种鸟类上端到端训练的分类器。通用基础模型再次恢复出了系统发育信号（AST r=0.55，CLAP r=0.52）。出乎意料的结果是，无论是BirdNET还是生物声学领域的BEATs-bio均未能超越它们（r约0.32至0.36）。将训练领域与目标类群相匹配本身并无帮助。预训练音频嵌入在两个独立的辐射类群中携带了进化信息，且这种信息的涌现并不需要领域特定的预训练。

</details>


### [145] [Complexity Bounds and Approaches to Learning Projected Gradient Descent Solver Iterates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22467)
*Anjian Li,Ryne Beeson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种k-邻域数据收集策略，通过将求解器的中间迭代步纳入训练集来缓解参数优化问题中数据稀缺的挑战，并基于Rademacher复杂度推导了泛化界以理论化其优势，最后与GLENS全局搜索方法建立了联系。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 参数优化问题通常数值求解代价高昂，而训练生成模型需要大量数据，数据稀缺成为根本性挑战。现有方法需要多次完整求解器运行才能获得足够训练数据，效率低下。

Method: 提出k-邻域数据收集策略：将求解器（如投影梯度下降法）在收敛过程中的中间迭代步作为额外训练数据，从而在不额外增加求解器运行次数的情况下扩充数据集。以单边盒约束二次规划为研究对象，通过Rademacher复杂度推导泛化界，分析该策略的理论优势。

Result: 推导了基于Rademacher复杂度的泛化界，揭示了k-邻域及相关参数对泛化性能的影响；通过两个示例展示了投影梯度下降求解器的行为；该方法能够提升DDDAS范式中数据-模型-优化循环的效率。

Conclusion: k-邻域数据收集策略能够有效缓解数据稀缺问题，提升生成模型在参数优化中的初始猜测质量；该工作增强了DDDAS范式的实用性；最后讨论了学习求解器迭代数据的两种视角，并将分析结果与GLENS全局搜索方法相连接。

Original Abstract: Data scarcity poses a fundamental challenge in training generative models to produce initial guesses for parametric optimization problems that are otherwise numerically expensive to solve. We therefore study a $k$-neighborhood data collection strategy that augments datasets of converged solutions with intermediate solver iterates, increasing the amount of training data without additional solver runs. To understand the benefits of this approach, we derive a generalization bound based on Rademacher complexity that reveals the role of the $k$-neighborhoods and related parameters. To achieve this result, we focus on one-sided box-constrained quadratic programs solved by projected gradient descent. We illustrate the behavior of this solver on two examples. The approach proposed in this paper enables a more capable DDDAS paradigm by improving the efficiency of the data-model-optimization loop. We finish by discussing two views of learning solver-iterate data and connect our analysis with GLENS, a new data-efficient global search method.

中文摘要翻译: 数据稀缺对训练生成模型构成了根本性挑战，这些模型旨在为参数优化问题生成初始猜测，而此类问题通常数值求解代价高昂。为此，我们研究了一种k-邻域数据收集策略，该策略将求解器的中间迭代步纳入已收敛解的数据集中，从而在不增加额外求解器运行次数的前提下扩充训练数据量。为了理解该方法的优势，我们基于Rademacher复杂度推导了一个泛化界，揭示了k-邻域及相关参数的作用。为得到这一结果，我们聚焦于采用投影梯度下降法求解的单边盒约束二次规划问题。我们通过两个示例展示了该求解器的行为。本文提出的方法通过提升数据-模型-优化循环的效率，使得DDDAS范式更具实用性。最后，我们讨论了学习求解器迭代数据的两种视角，并将我们的分析与一种新的数据高效全局搜索方法GLENS联系起来。

</details>


### [146] [Beyond Negative-Ridge Endpoints: Mixed-Sign Spectral Regularization via Negative-Shifted Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22474)
*Peng Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究过参数化线性回归中，负向平移的梯度下降（negative-shifted gradient descent）如何通过早期停止克服稳定负脊端点的结构性限制，在高有效秩尾部模型下实现风险多项式因子的改善，并利用局部化Duhamel积分处理非收缩动力学的技术挑战。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 过参数化线性回归中，弱谱方向对信号谱产生类似脊惩罚的效果，而负脊（negative ridge）作为自然修正虽能将滤波器推至1以上，但其稳定端点存在结构性限制：极点必须保持在最小非零经验特征值以下，且对小特征值的反收缩弱于大特征值。为突破这一约束，需要探索更优的优化动态。

Method: 提出早期停止的负向移位梯度下降法（early-stopped negative-shifted gradient descent），其滤波器在潜在极点处光滑且支持混合符号能力。利用局部化Duhamel积分处理非收缩移位动力学这一核心技术挑战，并结合有限网格留出不等式将分离性传递到验证选择的算法上。

Result: 在高斯尖峰加平坦（spike-plus-flat）模型中发现了Marchenko-Pastur势垒：消除隐式惩罚的移位位于最小经验特征值之上一个谱宽处。早期停止路径在所有可容许端点上实现风险多项式因子的改善。主定理允许一般高有效秩尾部，其迹设定隐式下界，平方谱控制暴露度，下界临界路径可同时恢复所有头部尺度。

Conclusion: 早期停止的负向移位梯度下降能够有效突破稳定负脊端点的结构性限制，在过参数化线性回归中实现更优的统计性能，为非收缩动力学分析提供了新的技术框架。

Original Abstract: In overparameterized linear regression, many weak spectral directions act like a ridge penalty on the signal-bearing spectrum; negative ridge is the natural correction, pushing filters above one. The stable negative-ridge endpoint, however, is structurally limited: its pole must stay below the smallest nonzero empirical eigenvalue, and it anti-shrinks smaller eigenvalues more than larger ones. Early-stopped negative-shifted gradient descent escapes this constraint. Its filter is smooth at the would-be pole and mixed-sign-capable: above-ridgeless directions form a leading prefix, with lower directions shrunk or exposure-controlled while stopping sets the crossover. In a Gaussian spike-plus-flat model we discover a Marchenko-Pastur barrier: the shift that cancels the implicit penalty lies a bulk width above the smallest empirical eigenvalue, and the stopped path improves on every admissible endpoint by a polynomial factor in risk under explicit conditions. Our main theorem permits a general high-effective-rank tail: its trace sets the implicit floor, its squared spectrum controls exposure, and the floor-critical path recovers all head scales at once, beyond positive shrinkage and, once scales separate, every uniform rescaling of ridgeless. Handling the noncontractive shifted dynamics is the central technical challenge; localized Duhamel integrals control them. A finite-grid hold-out inequality transfers the separations to the validation-selected algorithm.

中文摘要翻译: 在过参数化线性回归中，许多弱谱方向对信号谱的作用类似于脊惩罚；负脊是自然的修正，将滤波器推至1以上。然而，稳定的负脊端点存在结构性限制：其极点必须保持在最小非零经验特征值以下，且对小特征值的反收缩弱于大特征值。早期停止的负向移位梯度下降法突破了这一约束。其滤波器在潜在极点处光滑且支持混合符号能力：高于无脊方向的信号形成主导前缀，较低方向则被收缩或暴露受控，同时停止时机设定交叉点。在高斯尖峰加平坦模型中，我们发现了一个Marchenko-Pastur势垒：消除隐式惩罚的移位位于最小经验特征值之上一个谱宽处，且停止路径在显式条件下对所有可容许端点实现风险多项式因子的改善。我们的主定理允许一般高有效秩尾部：其迹设定隐式下界，其平方谱控制暴露度，而下界临界路径可同时恢复所有头部尺度——超越正收缩，且一旦尺度分离，即可恢复无脊方向的每个均匀缩放。处理非收缩移位动力学是核心技术挑战；局部化Duhamel积分对其加以控制。一个有限网格留出不等式将这些分离性传递到验证选择的算法上。

</details>


### [147] [\k{appa}-LoRA: Condition Numbers Reveal Which LoRA Matrices Worth Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22489)
*Jianghui Wang,Silong Yong,Francesco Orabona,Marco Canini,Katia P. Sycara,Yaqi Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出κ-LoRA，通过仅更新条件数最大的50%权重矩阵来减少LoRA的计算开销，在保持精度的同时将可训练参数减半，微调时间平均缩短16.2%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准LoRA均匀更新所有矩阵，计算成本高，尤其在大规模模型和资源受限场景（如边缘部署、设备端微调）中问题突出。本文首次发现并非所有LoRA矩阵都值得同等更新。

Method: 提出κ-LoRA，根据条件数（最大与最小奇异值之比）对权重矩阵进行排序，仅更新条件数最大的前50%矩阵，其余矩阵保持不变，从而减少可训练参数和计算开销。

Result: 将可训练参数减半；微调时间平均缩短16.2%；内存成本降低4.5%；精度与标准LoRA持平；被选矩阵的条件数在训练过程中持续下降，表明方法有效性源于针对性的谱重平衡。

Conclusion: 基于条件数的选择性微调是有效的：条件数大的矩阵包含更丰富子空间并驱动性能提升，κ-LoRA通过聚焦更新这些矩阵实现了计算效率与精度的良好权衡。

Original Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become a widely adopted technique for efficient neural network fine-tuning, decomposing model updates into low-rank matrices. However, LoRA remains computationally costly because it updates all matrices uniformly, regardless of their actual contribution to adaptation. This cost is especially prohibitive for large-scale models with billions of parameters and for resource-constrained settings such as edge deployment and on-device fine-tuning. We show for the first time that not all LoRA matrices are equally worth tuning: matrices with smaller condition numbers (the ratio of largest to smallest singular value) are already well-balanced across directions and contribute only marginally to adaptation, whereas matrices with larger condition numbers contain underdeveloped directions that span richer subspaces and drive most of the performance gains. This observation itself is a key contribution of our work, and it motivates a more selective approach to fine-tuning. Building on this insight, we propose \k{appa}-LoRA, a method that optimizes LoRA by focusing updates on the matrices with the largest condition numbers, which capture the most informative directions of change. By restricting LoRA updates to the top 50% of weight matrices ranked by condition number, \k{appa}-LoRA halves the trainable parameter count and correspondingly reduces compute and memory cost. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks show that this design cuts fine-tuning time by 16.2% on average while matching the accuracy of standard LoRA and reducing memory cost by 4.5%. Further analysis reveals that the condition numbers of the selected matrices consistently decrease over training, suggesting that \k{appa}-LoRA's effectiveness stems from targeted spectral rebalancing rather than parameter selection alone.

中文摘要翻译: 低秩适配（LoRA）已成为一种广泛采用的神经网络高效微调技术，它将模型更新分解为低秩矩阵。然而，LoRA仍然计算成本高昂，因为它均匀地更新所有矩阵，而不考虑它们对适配的实际贡献。对于具有数十亿参数的大规模模型以及边缘部署和设备端微调等资源受限场景，这种成本尤其令人望而却步。我们首次证明，并非所有LoRA矩阵都同样值得微调：条件数（最大与最小奇异值的比值）较小的矩阵已经在各个方向上保持良好平衡，对适配的贡献微乎其微；而条件数较大的矩阵包含欠发展方向，能跨越更丰富的子空间，并驱动大部分性能提升。这一发现本身是我们工作的关键贡献，并启发了一种更具选择性的微调方法。基于这一见解，我们提出了κ-LoRA，该方法通过聚焦更新条件数最大的矩阵来优化LoRA，这些矩阵捕获了信息量最丰富的变化方向。通过将LoRA更新限制在按条件数排序的前50%权重矩阵上，κ-LoRA将可训练参数数量减半，并相应地降低了计算和内存成本。跨多个基准的大规模实验表明，这种设计平均将微调时间缩短了16.2%，同时匹配标准LoRA的准确率，并将内存成本降低了4.5%。进一步分析表明，被选矩阵的条件数在训练过程中持续下降，这意味着κ-LoRA的有效性源于针对性的谱重平衡，而不仅仅是参数选择。

</details>


### [148] [Susceptible Reservoir Architectures for Regime-Conditional Volatility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22491)
*Aliaksei Kaliutau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于储层计算原理的波动率预测架构SUSA，包括复值开链/周期储层和状态条件专家模型，在16个美国股票/ETF数据上实现了与GARCH相当甚至更优的QLIKE表现，并与HARQ模型形成互补集成效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 波动率预测中，持久性和测量噪声主导了预测信号，留给非线性模型可利用的结构信息非常有限，因此需要设计一种能有效提取残差结构的新型架构。

Method: 提出Susceptible Architectures (SUSA)储层设计原则，包括两种具体实现：基于复值开链和周期储层，以及基于状态条件专家模型（区分平静、爆发、恢复和持续压力四种状态）；同时实现Qiskit中的开放系统q-qubit版本；所有模型使用共同的AR-Ridge锚点和有界残差校正（QLIKE损失）。

Result: 在16个美国股票/ETF序列上，使用12步输入窗口和5步预测 horizon，模型在IWM和XLP上取得了统计显著的QLIKE改进；与HARQ模型集成后，堆叠集成比最强单一模型平均QLIKE提升0.0116，在75%的测试场景中获胜。

Conclusion: 提出的SUSA架构在波动率预测中具有竞争力，能够捕捉GARCH等传统模型未能充分利用的残差结构，并且与HARQ模型存在互补性，集成后可显著提升预测性能。

Original Abstract: Volatility forecasting is dominated by persistence and measurement noise, leaving limited residual structure for nonlinear models to exploit. We introduce Susceptible Architectures (SUSA), a reservoir-design principle for volatility forecasting, and its two concrete implementations, based on complex-valued open-chain and periodic reservoirs and regime-conditioned experts to interpret reservoir features across calm, onset, recovery, and persistent-stress states. We also implement open-system $q$-qubit counterparts in Qiskit while retaining a common AR-Ridge anchor and a bounded residual correction trained under QLIKE. We evaluate models on 16 U.S. equity and exchange-traded-fund series using three disjoint chronological training, validation, and test folds, a 12-observation input window, and a five-observation forecast horizon. The proposed models perform competitively with GARCH, achieving statistically significant QLIKE improvements for specific assets (IWM, XLP). Also models' forecasts complement HARQ-style predictions: a stacked ensemble improves mean QLIKE by 0.0116 over its strongest constituent and wins in 75% of test scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 波动率预测主要受持久性和测量噪声主导，留给非线性模型可利用的残差结构十分有限。本文提出了Susceptible Architectures（SUSA）——一种用于波动率预测的储层设计原则，及其两种具体实现，分别基于复值开链储层和周期储层，以及状态条件专家模型来解释跨平静、爆发、恢复和持续压力四种状态的储层特征。我们还在Qiskit中实现了开放系统q-qubit版本，同时保留了共同的AR-Ridge锚点和在QLIKE损失下训练的有界残差校正。我们在16个美国股票和ETF序列上评估模型，使用三个不重叠的时间顺序训练集、验证集和测试集，12个观测值的输入窗口和5个观测值的预测horizon。所提出的模型在与GARCH的竞争中表现相当，在特定资产（IWM、XLP）上取得了统计显著的QLIKE改进。此外，模型的预测与HARQ风格的预测具有互补性：堆叠集成在平均QLIKE上比其最强单一组成模型提升0.0116，并在75%的测试场景中获胜。

</details>


### [149] [Dysphagia Risk Stratification in Head and Neck Cancer via Two-Stage PRO-Clinical Stacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22514)
*Siyuan Zhao,Eric Ababio Anyimadu,Zachary G. Brumm,Yue Ma,Clifton David Fuller,Xinhua Zhang,G. Elisabeta Marai,Guadalupe Canahuate*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需视频荧光成像的吞咽障碍风险预测框架，通过整合患者报告结局（PRO）与结构化临床变量，使用两阶段堆叠模型在头颈癌幸存者中进行吞咽毒性风险分层。研究发现单个MDADI问题比总分更具预测信息，模型可实现无影像学检查的临床筛查。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 吞咽困难是头颈癌治疗后的严重并发症，但其金标准评估（视频荧光成像）需要专业设备、人员且患者负担重，难以在常规随访中推广。患者报告结局（PRO）虽低成本易采集，但缺乏明确的临床框架将PRO响应转化为可操作的分诊决策。本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出一种单次就诊的PRO-临床预测框架，采用两阶段堆叠模型（two-stage stacking model）：第一阶段利用个体MDADI问题响应及结构化临床变量提取特征，第二阶段将两源信息融合为统一的、可解释的风险评估模型，无需视频荧光成像输入。

Result: 1) 单个MDADI问题响应包含比复合总分或全局汇总分数更多的预测信息；2) 可解释性分析揭示了与吞咽障碍相关的症状模式及临床风险因素；3) 该框架实现了无影像学检查的吞咽障碍风险分层。

Conclusion: 结构化PRO-临床信息整合可作为头颈癌幸存者吞咽障碍风险分层的实用、无影像学检查手段，支持将PRO响应纳入临床分诊决策路径。

Original Abstract: Dysphagia is a debilitating late effect of head and neck cancer (HNC) treatment, yet timely identification of at-risk patients remains challenging in survivorship care. Definitive assessment relies on videofluoroscopic imaging, as captured by the Dynamic Imaging Grade of Swallowing Toxicity (CTCAE-DIGEST), which, while validated, requires specialized equipment, trained personnel, and significant patient burden, limiting its routine use in surveillance. Patient-reported outcomes (PROs), by contrast, are low-cost, scalable, and easily collected at any clinical encounter, making them an attractive alternative signal for identifying patients who may warrant further evaluation. However, a clear clinical framework for translating PRO responses into actionable interventions is still evolving. In particular, uncertainty remains regarding when a patient's self-reported symptom burden should prompt escalation of care.
  This study addresses this gap by formulating a single-visit PRO-clinical prediction framework and introducing a clinically interpretable two-stage stacking model to predict swallowing impairment risk using PRO responses and structured clinical variables, without requiring videofluoroscopic imaging. The proposed framework quantifies the independent contributions of patient-reported symptoms and clinical factors within a unified and interpretable risk assessment model. Our findings demonstrate that individual MDADI responses contain predictive information beyond that captured by composite or global summary scores, while interpretability analyses reveal symptom patterns and clinical risk factors associated with swallowing impairment. Together, these results support the use of structured PRO-clinical integration as a practical, imaging-free approach for dysphagia risk stratification in HNC survivorship.

中文摘要翻译: 吞咽困难是头颈癌（HNC）治疗后一种令人衰弱的晚期效应，然而在幸存者护理中及时识别高风险患者仍然具有挑战性。确定性评估依赖于视频荧光成像，即通过吞咽毒性动态成像分级（CTCAE-DIGEST）进行捕获；该方法虽然经过验证，但需要专业设备、训练有素的人员且患者负担沉重，限制了其在常规监测中的应用。相比之下，患者报告结局（PRO）成本低、可扩展性强且易于在任何临床接触中收集，使其成为识别可能需要进一步评估的患者的理想替代信号。然而，将PRO响应转化为可操作干预措施的清晰临床框架仍在发展之中。特别是，当患者的自报症状负担应促使升级护理时，仍存在不确定性。本研究通过构建单次就诊的PRO-临床预测框架来填补这一空白，并引入一种临床可解释的两阶段堆叠模型，利用PRO响应和结构化临床变量预测吞咽障碍风险，无需视频荧光成像。所提出的框架在统一且可解释的风险评估模型中量化了患者报告症状与临床因素的独立贡献。我们的研究结果表明，个体MDADI响应包含的信息超越了复合总分或全局汇总分数，而可解释性分析揭示了与吞咽障碍相关的症状模式和临床风险因素。综上，这些结果支持将结构化PRO-临床整合作为头颈癌幸存者吞咽障碍风险分层的实用、无影像学检查方法。

</details>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [Computer Vision Based Neurology Brain Activity Rejection Architecture and Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21654)
*Zag ElSayed,Nathan Suer,Grace Westerkamp,Jack Yanchen Liu,Makoto Miyakoshi,Craig Erickson,Ernest Pedapati*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于计算机视觉的自动化ICA伪迹剔除标注工具，可将处理时间缩短7200倍，准确率达到89.45%，适用于大规模EEG研究。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统ICA分解需要人工检查、选择和解释独立成分（ICs），耗时且需要专业知识，限制了大规模EEG研究和实时应用的发展。

Method: 提出一种基于计算机视觉的自动化ICA伪迹剔除标注工具，兼容ICLabel和EEGLab等常用软件接口，通过自动化替代人工标注过程。

Result: 处理时间缩短7200倍，准确率达到89.45%。

Conclusion: 自动化IC分类能够达到足够高的准确率，可加速大规模EEG研究，并在与脑活动伪迹剔除任务结合时实现近实时应用，对医学专家具有重要价值。

Original Abstract: The electroencephalogram (EEG) is a valuable and widely applied tool for investigating brain disorders and behavioral changes. It offers a minimally restrictive and non-invasive method. However, challenges in using EEG for cognitive development studies include temporal resolution, signal source localization, and EEG artifacts. Careful consideration of these factors is essential for informed application of EEG technology. Independent component analysis (ICA) effectively isolates source generator processes from signals recorded by multiple, adjacent EEG scalp electrodes. Although ICA decomposition requires manual inspection, selection, and interpretation of independent components (ICs), this process is time consuming and demands expertise. Automated IC classification can achieve sufficient accuracy, expediting large scale EEG research and enabling near real time applications in conjunction with brain activity rejection tasks, which are crucial for medical specialists. This study introduces an automated computer vision based ICA rejection labeling tool compatible with widely used software interfaces like ICLabel and EEGLab. By automating the manual task, the proposed system reduces processing time by 7200 fold and achieves an accuracy of 89.45%.

中文摘要翻译: 脑电图（EEG）是研究脑部疾病和行为变化的一种有价值且广泛应用的工具。它提供了一种最小限制性且无创的方法。然而，将EEG用于认知发展研究时面临的挑战包括时间分辨率、信号源定位以及EEG伪影。仔细考虑这些因素对于合理应用EEG技术至关重要。独立成分分析（ICA）能够有效地从多个相邻EEG头皮电极记录的信号中分离出源生成过程。尽管ICA分解需要人工检查、选择和解释独立成分（ICs），但这一过程耗时且需要专业知识。自动化IC分类可以达到足够的准确率，从而加速大规模EEG研究，并实现与脑活动伪迹剔除任务相结合的近实时应用，这对医学专家至关重要。本研究引入了一种基于计算机视觉的自动化ICA伪迹剔除标注工具，兼容ICLabel和EEGLab等常用软件接口。通过自动化人工任务，所提出的系统将处理时间缩短了7200倍，准确率达到89.45%。

</details>


### [151] [Cycles of Discourse, Speech Dysfluency, and Active Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22180)
*Thomas Parr,Birtan Demirel,Youssuf Saleh,Sanjay Manohar*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(POMDP)的语音产生与听觉分割计算模型，用于模拟和测试言语流畅性障碍（如口吃和神经退行性疾病中的言语障碍）的计算机制，并引入社交推断成分来区分自我与他人说话。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 开发一个计算模型作为"体外"测试平台，用于验证关于言语流畅性丧失机制的各种假设——这种丧失可能暂时发生在口吃者身上，或渐进性地出现在多种神经退行性疾病中。

Method: 基于部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(POMDP)构建计算模型，核心特征包括：(1)从词汇到变长音素序列的互逆映射；(2)语音生成与感知共享共同状态子集；(3)引入社交组件，推断说话者身份（自我或他人）。

Result: 证明降低下一个词的精度会导致停顿和词首重复等类似口吃的行为模式；检验了每种假设所预测的行为模式和信念更新规律，涵盖说话和倾听两方面。

Conclusion: 该模型能够模拟不同计算机制下的言语障碍行为模式，为理解口吃和神经退行性言语障碍的计算神经机制提供了理论框架，并指出了可通过功能成像进行验证的神经 substrates。

Original Abstract: Speech is a complex motor and social act. We must not only produce and understand sequences of variable-length words formed of ordered syllables but also speak and listen in turn. This theoretical paper introduces a computational model of speech production and auditory segmentation based upon sequences of discrete phonemes. The purpose of this is to develop a vehicle to test (in silico) hypotheses about the mechanisms that govern loss of speech fluency, something that may happen transiently in people who stutter or progressively in various neurodegenerative conditions. The modelling here appeals to Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes that provide a useful generic formulation for specifying the internal model our brains might use to describe dynamic environments in which they can exert partial control and make partial observations. The core features of the specific model used here are: (1) a reciprocal mapping from words to sequences of varying numbers of phonemes (2) a common subset of states for both speech generation and speech perception. A further novel feature is a social component, in which one must infer who is speaking (self or other). We propose a series of hypotheses about the computational mechanisms that might underwrite speech breakdown. For example, we demonstrate that reducing the precision in the next word introduces pauses and start-of-word repetitions of the sort associated with stuttering. We examine the patterns of behaviour and belief-updating predicted by each of these hypotheses, with implications for both speaking and listening. Finally, we discuss plausible neural substrates for the underlying message passing that might be amenable to interrogation with functional imaging.

中文摘要翻译: 言语是一种复杂的运动与社交行为。我们不仅需要生成和理解由有序音节构成的变长词序列，还需要轮流进行说话和倾听。本文提出一个基于离散音素序列的语音产生与听觉分割的计算模型，旨在开发一个"体外"测试工具，用于验证关于言语流畅性丧失机制的假设——这种丧失可能暂时发生在口吃者身上，或渐进性地出现在多种神经退行性疾病中。该模型采用部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程，它提供了一个通用的形式化框架，用于描述我们大脑可能用于刻画动态环境的内部模型，在这些环境中我们可以施加部分控制并进行部分观测。本模型的核心特征包括：(1)从词汇到不同长度音素序列的互逆映射；(2)语音生成与语音感知共享一个共同的状态子集。另一个新颖特征是社交组件，即需要推断说话者是谁（自我或他人）。我们提出了一系列关于可能构成言语障碍基础的计算机制的假设。例如，我们证明降低下一个词的精度会引发类似口吃的停顿和词首重复。我们检验了每种假设所预测的行为模式和信念更新规律，这对说话和倾听均有启示。最后，我们讨论了可能支撑底层消息传递的神经 substrates，这些神经基础可通过功能成像技术进行探究。

</details>
